132: @dataclass(frozen=True)
133: class ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
134:     target_path: Path
135:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
136:     lineage: str
137:     basis: ReadBasis
138:     captured_head: Head
139:     observer: str
140:     reviewed_text: str
141:     reviewed_sha256: str
142:     declaration_text: str
143:     declaration_sha256: str
144:     documents: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...]
145:     draft: RecordDraft
146:     exact_drafts: str
147:     credential_bindings: tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]
148: 
149:     @property
150:     def fact_ids(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
151:         return (self.lineage,)
152: 
153: 
154: @dataclass(frozen=True)
155: class ArrivalAdoptionResult:
156:     status: str
157:     target_path: Path
158:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor
159:     lineage: str
160:     basis: ReadBasis
161:     captured_head: Head
162:     head: Head
163:     commit: Commit | None
164:     fact_ids: tuple[str, ...]
165:     intent_path: Path | None
166:     phase: str
167:     file_written: bool
168:     projection: ProjectionSyncResult | None
169:     credential_bindings: tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]
170:     observed_head: Head | None = None
171: 
172: 
173: def arrival_adoption_intent_path(target: Path | str) -> Path:
174:     path = Path(target).resolve()
175:     return path.with_name(path.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent")
176: 
177: 
178: def _close(handle: object | None) -> None:
179:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
180:     if callable(close):
181:         with suppress(Exception):
182:             close()
183: 
184: 
185: def _cache_bytes(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan) -> bytes:
186:     return plan.declaration_text.encode("utf-8")
187: 
188: 
189: def _require_cache(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan) -> None:
190:     try:
191:         actual = plan.target_path.read_bytes()
192:     except OSError as exc:
193:         raise AdoptionStale(f"reviewed declaration cache is unavailable: {exc}") from exc
194:     if (
195:         actual != _cache_bytes(plan)
196:         or hashlib.sha256(actual).hexdigest() != plan.declaration_sha256
197:     ):
198:         raise AdoptionStale("reviewed declaration bytes changed")
199: 
200: 
201: def _selected_documents(
202:     text: str, target: Path, descriptor: StoreDescriptor
203: ) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], ...]:
204:     try:
205:         ast = parse_vertex(text, path=target)
206:         validate_vertex(ast)
207:         validate_arrival_runtime_identity(ast.name, ast.loops, refusal=AdoptionPreparationRefused)
208:         backend = ast.store_backend
209:         if (
210:             backend is None or backend.name != descriptor.backend
211:             or backend.lineage != descriptor.lineage
212:             or backend.role != Profile.AUTHORITY.value
213:         ):
214:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
215:                 "reviewed declaration residence differs from authority descriptor"
216:             )
217:         location = (
218:             str(canonical_store_path(ast.store, target))
219:             if descriptor.backend == "file" and ast.store is not None
220:             else ast.store_location
221:         )
222:         if location != descriptor.location:
223:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
224:                 "reviewed declaration store location differs from authority descriptor"
225:             )
226:         documents = tuple(document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(ast))
227:         seen: set[tuple[str, str]] = set()
228:         for document in documents:
229:             identity = (document["kind"], document["subject"])
230:             if identity in seen:
231:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"duplicate declaration document {identity!r}")
232:             seen.add(identity)
233:         return documents
234:     except AdoptionPreparationRefused:
235:         raise
236:     except Exception as exc:
237:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"reviewed declaration is invalid: {exc}") from exc
238: 
239: 
240: def _scan_identity(records: tuple[Mapping[str, Any], ...], lineage: str) -> None:
241:     for record in records:
242:         if record["k"] not in {"fact", "batch"}:
243:             continue
244:         for kind, row in rows_of_body(record["k"], record["body"]):
245:             if kind != "fact":
246:                 continue
247:             fact_id, fact_kind, _at, _observer, _origin, payload, _signature = row
248:             if fact_id == lineage:
249:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
250:                     "physical-lineage fact ID already occurs in captured prefix"
251:                 )
252:             if not fact_kind.startswith("_decl."):
253:                 continue
254:             try:
255:                 data = json.loads(payload)
256:             except ValueError:
257:                 continue
258:             if isinstance(data, dict) and data.get("lineage") == lineage:
259:                 raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
260:                     "historical declaration overlay claims physical lineage"
261:                 )
262: 
263: 
264: def prepare_arrival_adoption(
265:     registry: BackendRegistry,
266:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
267:     *, target: Path | str, selected_head: Head,
268:     reviewed_text: str, reviewed_sha256: str, declaration_text: str,
269:     observer: str, credentials: WriteCredentials,
270:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
271:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
272:     authored_at: float | None = None,
273: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
274:     """Capture Full(S), verify the migration registry and sign one exact anchor."""
275:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
276:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY or not descriptor.lineage:
277:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires a lineage-pinned Authority descriptor")
278:     if not isinstance(selected_head, Head) or selected_head.lineage != descriptor.lineage:
279:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("selected head must name the descriptor lineage")
280:     if not observer or not isinstance(observer, str):
281:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adopting observer must be explicit")
282:     if not credentials.mapped or credentials.signature_verifier is None:
283:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires pre-created mapped credentials")
284:     if not callable(fact_verify) or not callable(arrival_verify):
285:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adoption requires public FACT and ARRIVAL verifiers")
286:     if not all(
287:         isinstance(value, str)
288:         for value in (reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256, declaration_text)
289:     ):
290:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("reviewed and published declaration text are required")
291:     reviewed = reviewed_text.encode("utf-8")
292:     if hashlib.sha256(reviewed).hexdigest() != reviewed_sha256:
293:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("reviewed snapshot SHA-256 differs from selected text")
294:     published = declaration_text.encode("utf-8")
295:     try:
296:         if target_path.read_bytes() != published:
297:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
298:                 "published declaration bytes differ from supplied authority text"
299:             )
300:     except OSError as exc:
301:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"copied declaration is unavailable: {exc}") from exc
302:     documents = _selected_documents(declaration_text, target_path, descriptor)
303:     try:
304:         reviewed_ast = parse_vertex(reviewed_text, path=target_path)
305:         validate_vertex(reviewed_ast)
306:         reviewed_documents = tuple(
307:             document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(reviewed_ast)
308:         )
309:     except Exception as exc:
310:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"reviewed source snapshot is invalid: {exc}") from exc
311:     if reviewed_documents != documents:
312:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
313:             "published authority declaration differs from reviewed source documents"
314:         )
315:     ledger = query = None
316:     try:
317:         ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
318:         captured = ledger.head()
319:         if captured != selected_head or ledger.verify(Full(through=selected_head)) != selected_head:
320:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
321:                 "selected migration head is stale or not Full-verified"
322:             )
323:         records = tuple(ledger.scan(through=selected_head))
324:         _scan_identity(records, captured.lineage)
325:         keys, _evidence = key_registry_from_records(records, arrival_verify)
326:         valid = tuple(key for key, _ordinal in keys.keys_valid_at(observer, captured.ordinal + 1))
327:         if not valid:
328:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("adopting observer has no key valid at selected head")
329:         for document in documents:
330:             if document["kind"] != DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED:
331:                 continue
332:             key = document["payload"].get("key")
333:             if key is not None:
334:                 named = document["subject"]
335:                 named_valid = {
336:                     item for item, _ordinal in keys.keys_valid_at(named, captured.ordinal + 1)
337:                 }
338:                 if key not in named_valid:
339:                     raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
340:                         f"declared observer {named!r} key is not valid at selected head"
341:                     )
342:         if ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records == 0:
343:             raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("backend cannot append an adoption anchor")
344:     except AdoptionPreparationRefused:
345:         raise
346:     except Exception as exc:
347:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"cannot establish adoption prefix: {exc}") from exc
348:     finally:
349:         _close(query)
350:         _close(ledger)
351: 
352:     try:
353:         preflight_projection_maintenance(registry, descriptor, through=captured)
354:     except Exception as exc:
355:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
356:             f"required projection maintenance is unavailable at S: {exc}"
357:         ) from exc
358: 
359:     session = CredentialResolutionSession()
360:     context = CapturedSigningContext(captured, ((observer, valid),), ())
361:     namespace = credentials.binding_namespace
362:     assert namespace is not None
363:     def _sign(domain: SigningDomain) -> Callable[[str, str], str | None]:
364:         def sign(named: str, digest: str) -> str | None:
365:             request = CredentialRequest(namespace, named, domain, CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP)
366:             return session.sign(
367:                 credentials, request, digest, context=context,
368:                 authorized_keys=context.keys_for_author(named), required=True,
369:             )
370:         return sign
371:     at = time.time() if authored_at is None else authored_at
372:     try:
373:         draft = build_declaration_anchor_draft(
374:             lineage=captured.lineage, authored_at=at, observer=observer,
375:             documents=documents, fact_signer=_sign(SigningDomain.FACT),
376:             arrival_signer=_sign(SigningDomain.ARRIVAL),
377:         )
378:     except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
379:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"mapped adoption binding refused: {exc}") from exc
380:     except Exception as exc:
381:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(f"cannot sign adoption anchor: {exc}") from exc
382:     bindings = session.evidence
383:     if (
384:         len(bindings) != 2
385:         or bindings[0].request.domain is not SigningDomain.FACT
386:         or bindings[1].request.domain is not SigningDomain.ARRIVAL
387:         or bindings[0].public_key != bindings[1].public_key
388:         or bindings[0].public_key not in valid
389:     ):
390:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("mapped adoption bindings disagree")
391:     bound_key = bindings[0].public_key
392:     if not isinstance(draft.signature, str) or not isinstance(
393:         draft.body.get("signature"), str
394:     ):
395:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused("draft signatures are missing")
396:     inner_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
397:         "_decl.genesis", at, observer, "", draft.body["payload"]
398:     )
399:     outer_digest = content_commitment("fact", at, observer, "", dict(draft.body))
400:     try:
401:         fact_ok = fact_verify(bound_key, draft.body["signature"], inner_digest)
402:         arrival_ok = arrival_verify(bound_key, draft.signature, outer_digest)
403:     except Exception as exc:
404:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
405:             f"independent adoption signature verification failed: {exc}"
406:         ) from exc
407:     if not fact_ok or not arrival_ok:
408:         raise AdoptionPreparationRefused(
409:             "draft signatures do not verify under the selected mapped public key"
410:         )
411:     return ArrivalAdoptionPlan(
412:         target_path, descriptor, captured.lineage,
413:         ReadBasis(captured.lineage, captured, None, None), captured, observer,
414:         reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256, declaration_text,
415:         hashlib.sha256(published).hexdigest(), documents, draft,
416:         _exact_drafts((draft,)), bindings,
417:     )
418: 
419: 
420: def _intent_data(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan, phase: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
421:     return {
422:         "schema": _SCHEMA, "phase": phase, "target": str(plan.target_path),
423:         "descriptor": _descriptor_dict(plan.descriptor),
424:         "captured_head": _head_dict(plan.captured_head),
425:         "observer": plan.observer, "reviewed_text": plan.reviewed_text,
426:         "reviewed_sha256": plan.reviewed_sha256,
427:         "declaration_text": plan.declaration_text,
428:         "declaration_sha256": plan.declaration_sha256,
429:         "drafts": json.loads(plan.exact_drafts), "exact_drafts": plan.exact_drafts,
430:         "bindings": [
431:             {"request": {"namespace": evidence.request.namespace,
432:                          "observer": evidence.request.observer,
433:                          "domain": evidence.request.domain.value,
434:                          "purpose": evidence.request.purpose.value},
435:              "key_ref": evidence.key_ref, "algorithm": evidence.algorithm,
436:              "public_key": evidence.public_key, "provenance": evidence.provenance}
437:             for evidence in plan.credential_bindings
438:         ],
439:     }
440: 
441: 
442: def _result(plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan, status: str, head: Head,
443:             commit: Commit | None, projection: ProjectionSyncResult | None,
444:             phase: str, file_written: bool,
445:             observed_head: Head | None = None) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
446:     return ArrivalAdoptionResult(
447:         status, plan.target_path, plan.descriptor, plan.lineage, plan.basis,
448:         plan.captured_head, head, commit, plan.fact_ids, None, phase,
449:         file_written, projection, plan.credential_bindings, observed_head,
450:     )
451: 
452: 
453: def _finish(registry: BackendRegistry, plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
454:             intent: Path, data: dict[str, Any], head: Head,
455:             commit: Commit | None, status: str,
456:             failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None,
457:             observed_head: Head | None = None) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
458:     projection = None
459:     try:
460:         projection = sync_projection(registry, plan.descriptor, through=head)
461:         if projection.projected_after.ordinal < head.ordinal:
462:             raise AdoptionApplyError("projection did not reach adoption head")
463:         data["phase"] = "synced"
464:         _replace_json(intent, data)
465:         if failure_hook:
466:             failure_hook("after-sync")
467:         _require_cache(plan)
468:         data["phase"] = "published"
469:         _replace_json(intent, data)
470:         if failure_hook:
471:             failure_hook("after-publication")
472:         _remove_intent(intent)
473:     except Exception as exc:
474:         raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
475:             "adoption committed but projection or cache reconciliation remains",
476:             phase=data["phase"], head=head, commit=commit,
477:             intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
478:         ) from exc
479:     return _result(
480:         plan, status, head, commit, projection, "published", False, observed_head
481:     )
482: 
483: 
484: def apply_arrival_adoption(
485:     registry: BackendRegistry, plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
486:     *, failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
487: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
488:     """Reserve the signed draft, CAS against S, then sync and reconcile cache."""
489:     with _declaration_lock(plan.target_path):
490:         if _exact_drafts((plan.draft,)) != plan.exact_drafts:
491:             raise AdoptionApplyError("prepared adoption draft changed")
492:         _require_cache(plan)
493:         intent = arrival_adoption_intent_path(plan.target_path)
494:         data = _intent_data(plan, "prepared")
495:         try:
496:             _write_exclusive_json(intent, data)
497:         except FileExistsError as exc:
498:             raise AdoptionApplyError(f"unfinished adoption intent exists: {intent}") from exc
499:         if failure_hook:
500:             try:
501:                 failure_hook("after-intent")
502:             except Exception as exc:
503:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
504:                     "adoption intent is durable before append; recover explicitly",
505:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
506:                 ) from exc
507:         ledger = query = None
508:         try:
509:             try:
510:                 ledger, query = registry.open(plan.descriptor)
511:                 current = ledger.head()
512:             except Exception as exc:
513:                 raise AdoptionRecoveryRequired(
514:                     "adoption intent is durable but custody could not be opened",
515:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
516:                 ) from exc
517:             if current != plan.captured_head:
518:                 raise AdoptionStale(
519:                     "migration head changed before adoption append; recover intent "
520:                     "to prove whether it is superseded"
521:                 )
522:             try:
523:                 commit = ledger.append(plan.captured_head, (plan.draft,))
524:             except Exception as exc:
525:                 from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
526:                 from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
527:                 if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
528:                     raise AdoptionStale(
529:                         f"adoption append refused: {exc}; recover intent to "
530:                         "establish its disposition"
531:                     ) from exc
532:                 if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
533:                     data["phase"] = "append-unwitnessed"
534:                     with suppress(Exception):
535:                         _replace_json(intent, data)
536:                     raise AdoptionUnwitnessed(
537:                         "adoption committed without a complete witness",
538:                         head=exc.head, commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
539:                     ) from exc
540:                 data["phase"] = "append-unknown"
541:                 with suppress(Exception):
542:                     _replace_json(intent, data)
543:                 raise AdoptionOutcomeUnknown(
544:                     "adoption append outcome is unknown", head=plan.captured_head,
545:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
546:                 ) from exc
547:             data["phase"] = "appended"
548:             try:
549:                 _replace_json(intent, data)
550:                 if failure_hook:
551:                     failure_hook("after-append")
552:             except Exception as exc:
553:                 raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
554:                     "adoption committed but append phase recording failed",
555:                     phase="appended", head=commit.after, commit=commit,
556:                     intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
557:                 ) from exc
558:         finally:
559:             _close(query)
560:             _close(ledger)
561:         return _finish(registry, plan, intent, data, commit.after, commit,
562:                        "applied", failure_hook)
563: 
564: 
565: def _intent_object(value: Any, label: str) -> Mapping[str, Any]:
566:     if not isinstance(value, dict):
567:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"adoption intent {label} must be an object")
568:     return value
569: 
570: 
571: def _intent_string(value: Any, label: str) -> str:
572:     if not isinstance(value, str) or not value:
573:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"adoption intent {label} must be a nonempty string")
574:     return value
575: 
576: 
577: def _plan_from_intent(data: Any) -> ArrivalAdoptionPlan:
578:     root = _intent_object(data, "root")
579:     if root.get("schema") != _SCHEMA:
580:         raise AdoptionApplyError("unsupported adoption intent")
581:     if root.get("phase") not in {
582:         "prepared", "append-unknown", "append-unwitnessed", "appended",
583:         "synced", "published",
584:     }:
585:         raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent phase is invalid")
586:     try:
587:         captured_data = _intent_object(root["captured_head"], "captured head")
588:         ordinal = captured_data["ordinal"]
589:         if not isinstance(ordinal, int) or isinstance(ordinal, bool) or ordinal < 0:
590:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent captured ordinal is invalid")
591:         captured = Head(
592:             _intent_string(captured_data["lineage"], "captured lineage"),
593:             ordinal,
594:             _intent_string(captured_data["record_hash"], "captured hash"),
595:         )
596:         descriptor_data = _intent_object(root["descriptor"], "descriptor")
597:         for field in ("backend", "location", "lineage", "role"):
598:             _intent_string(descriptor_data[field], f"descriptor {field}")
599:         descriptor = _descriptor_from(descriptor_data)
600:         target = Path(_intent_string(root["target"], "target")).resolve()
601:         text = _intent_string(root["declaration_text"], "authority text")
602:         digest = _intent_string(root["declaration_sha256"], "authority hash")
603:         reviewed_text = _intent_string(root["reviewed_text"], "reviewed text")
604:         reviewed_sha256 = _intent_string(root["reviewed_sha256"], "reviewed hash")
605:         observer = _intent_string(root["observer"], "observer")
606:         if hashlib.sha256(text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != digest:
607:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent declaration bytes do not verify")
608:         if hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest() != reviewed_sha256:
609:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent reviewed snapshot bytes do not verify")
610:         raw_drafts = root["drafts"]
611:         if not isinstance(raw_drafts, list):
612:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent drafts must be a list")
613:         exact_drafts = _intent_string(root["exact_drafts"], "exact drafts")
614:         drafts = _drafts_from_exact(raw_drafts, exact_drafts)
615:         if len(drafts) != 1 or drafts[0].body.get("id") != captured.lineage:
616:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent has no exact lineage anchor")
617:         raw_bindings = root["bindings"]
618:         if not isinstance(raw_bindings, list):
619:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent bindings must be a list")
620:         evidence = []
621:         for index, raw in enumerate(raw_bindings):
622:             item = _intent_object(raw, f"binding {index}")
623:             request = _intent_object(item["request"], f"binding {index} request")
624:             evidence.append(CredentialBindingEvidence(
625:                 CredentialRequest(
626:                     _intent_string(request["namespace"], "binding namespace"),
627:                     _intent_string(request["observer"], "binding observer"),
628:                     SigningDomain(_intent_string(request["domain"], "binding domain")),
629:                     CredentialPurpose(_intent_string(request["purpose"], "binding purpose")),
630:                 ),
631:                 _intent_string(item["key_ref"], "binding key ref"),
632:                 _intent_string(item["algorithm"], "binding algorithm"),
633:                 _intent_string(item["public_key"], "binding public key"),
634:                 _intent_string(item["provenance"], "binding provenance"),
635:             ))
636:         if descriptor.lineage != captured.lineage:
637:             raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent descriptor and captured lineage disagree")
638:         return ArrivalAdoptionPlan(
639:             target, descriptor, captured.lineage,
640:             ReadBasis(captured.lineage, captured, None, None), captured,
641:             observer, reviewed_text, reviewed_sha256,
642:             text, digest, (), drafts[0], exact_drafts, tuple(evidence),
643:         )
644:     except AdoptionApplyError:
645:         raise
646:     except Exception as exc:
647:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"malformed adoption intent: {exc}") from exc
648: 
649: 
650: def _revalidate_reserved(
651:     ledger: Any,
652:     plan: ArrivalAdoptionPlan,
653:     *,
654:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
655:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
656: ) -> None:
657:     """Prove a public recovery intent is the same authorized adoption proposal."""
658:     try:
659:         if plan.descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY or plan.descriptor.lineage != plan.lineage:
660:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved descriptor is not this lineage's Authority")
661:         if ledger.verify(Full(through=plan.captured_head)) != plan.captured_head:
662:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved predecessor is not Full-verified")
663:         records = tuple(ledger.scan(through=plan.captured_head))
664:         _scan_identity(records, plan.lineage)
665:         registry, _evidence = key_registry_from_records(records, arrival_verify)
666:         valid = {
667:             key for key, _ordinal in registry.keys_valid_at(
668:                 plan.observer, plan.captured_head.ordinal + 1
669:             )
670:         }
671:         if not valid:
672:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved author has no key valid at S")
673:         documents = _selected_documents(
674:             plan.declaration_text, plan.target_path, plan.descriptor
675:         )
676:         reviewed_ast = parse_vertex(plan.reviewed_text, path=plan.target_path)
677:         validate_vertex(reviewed_ast)
678:         reviewed_documents = tuple(
679:             document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(reviewed_ast)
680:         )
681:         if reviewed_documents != documents:
682:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved reviewed documents differ from authority form")
683:         for document in documents:
684:             if document["kind"] != DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED:
685:                 continue
686:             key = document["payload"].get("key")
687:             if key is not None and key not in {
688:                 item for item, _ordinal in registry.keys_valid_at(
689:                     document["subject"], plan.captured_head.ordinal + 1
690:                 )
691:             }:
692:                 raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved observer key was not valid at S")
693: 
694:         bindings = plan.credential_bindings
695:         if len(bindings) != 2:
696:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT and ARRIVAL binding evidence is incomplete")
697:         by_domain = {item.request.domain: item for item in bindings}
698:         if set(by_domain) != {SigningDomain.FACT, SigningDomain.ARRIVAL}:
699:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved binding domains are invalid or duplicated")
700:         fact_binding = by_domain[SigningDomain.FACT]
701:         arrival_binding = by_domain[SigningDomain.ARRIVAL]
702:         for item in bindings:
703:             if (
704:                 item.request.observer != plan.observer
705:                 or item.request.purpose is not CredentialPurpose.AUTHORSHIP
706:                 or not all((item.key_ref, item.algorithm, item.public_key, item.provenance))
707:                 or item.public_key not in valid
708:             ):
709:                 raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved binding is not authorized at S")
710:         if (
711:             fact_binding.request.namespace != arrival_binding.request.namespace
712:             or fact_binding.key_ref != arrival_binding.key_ref
713:             or fact_binding.public_key != arrival_binding.public_key
714:         ):
715:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT and ARRIVAL bindings disagree")
716: 
717:         draft = plan.draft
718:         payload = json.dumps(
719:             {"protocol": 1, "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents]},
720:             ensure_ascii=False, separators=(",", ":"),
721:         )
722:         body = draft.body
723:         if (
724:             draft.kind != "fact" or draft.observer != plan.observer
725:             or draft.origin != "" or body.get("id") != plan.lineage
726:             or body.get("kind") != "_decl.genesis"
727:             or body.get("ts") != draft.authored_at
728:             or body.get("observer") != plan.observer
729:             or body.get("origin") != "" or body.get("payload") != payload
730:             or not isinstance(body.get("signature"), str)
731:             or not isinstance(draft.signature, str)
732:         ):
733:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved declaration anchor differs from reviewed documents")
734:         expected_body = body_of_fact_row((
735:             plan.lineage, "_decl.genesis", draft.authored_at,
736:             plan.observer, "", payload, body["signature"],
737:         ))
738:         if dict(body) != expected_body:
739:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved declaration anchor has unexpected fields")
740:         fact_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
741:             "_decl.genesis", draft.authored_at, plan.observer, "", payload
742:         )
743:         arrival_digest = content_commitment(
744:             "fact", draft.authored_at, plan.observer, "", dict(body)
745:         )
746:         key = fact_binding.public_key
747:         if not fact_verify(key, body["signature"], fact_digest):
748:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved FACT signature does not verify")
749:         if not arrival_verify(key, draft.signature, arrival_digest):
750:             raise AdoptionApplyError("reserved ARRIVAL signature does not verify")
751:     except AdoptionApplyError:
752:         raise
753:     except Exception as exc:
754:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"cannot revalidate reserved adoption: {exc}") from exc
755: 
756: 
757: def recover_arrival_adoption(
758:     registry: BackendRegistry, intent: Path | str,
759:     *, fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
760:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool],
761:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
762: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
763:     """Reconcile the exact reserved row; never re-sign or append a duplicate."""
764:     intent_path = Path(intent).resolve()
765:     try:
766:         data = json.loads(intent_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
767:         plan = _plan_from_intent(data)
768:     except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError) as exc:
769:         raise AdoptionApplyError(f"cannot read adoption intent: {exc}") from exc
770:     if intent_path != arrival_adoption_intent_path(plan.target_path):
771:         raise AdoptionApplyError("adoption intent path does not match its target")
772:     if not callable(fact_verify) or not callable(arrival_verify):
773:         raise AdoptionApplyError("recovery requires public FACT and ARRIVAL verifiers")
774:     with _declaration_lock(plan.target_path):
775:         ledger = query = None
776:         try:
777:             ledger, query = registry.open(plan.descriptor)
778:             predecessor = ledger.head_at(Watermark(plan.lineage, plan.captured_head.ordinal))
779:             if predecessor != plan.captured_head:
780:                 raise AdoptionStale("adoption predecessor hash changed")
781:             _revalidate_reserved(
782:                 ledger, plan, fact_verify=fact_verify,
783:                 arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
784:             )
785:             current = ledger.head()
786:             observed_head = current
787:             if current == predecessor:
788:                 _require_cache(plan)
789:                 try:
790:                     commit = ledger.append(predecessor, (plan.draft,))
791:                 except Exception as exc:
792:                     from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
793:                     from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
794:                     if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
795:                         raise AdoptionStale(f"reserved adoption CAS refused: {exc}") from exc
796:                     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
797:                         raise AdoptionUnwitnessed(
798:                             "reserved adoption committed without a complete witness",
799:                             head=exc.head, commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent_path,
800:                             cause=exc,
801:                         ) from exc
802:                     raise AdoptionOutcomeUnknown(
803:                         "reserved adoption append outcome is unknown", head=predecessor,
804:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
805:                     ) from exc
806:                 current = commit.after
807:                 observed_head = current
808:                 data["phase"] = "appended"
809:                 try:
810:                     _replace_json(intent_path, data)
811:                     if failure_hook:
812:                         failure_hook("after-append")
813:                 except Exception as exc:
814:                     raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
815:                         "reserved adoption committed but phase recording failed",
816:                         phase="appended", head=current, commit=commit,
817:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
818:                     ) from exc
819:             else:
820:                 if current.ordinal < predecessor.ordinal + 1:
821:                     raise AdoptionStale("reserved adoption has no successor at S+1")
822:                 adoption_head = ledger.head_at(
823:                     Watermark(plan.lineage, predecessor.ordinal + 1)
824:                 )
825:                 if ledger.verify(Full(through=adoption_head)) != adoption_head:
826:                     raise AdoptionStale("reserved adoption suffix is not Full-verified")
827:                 successor = ledger.read(predecessor.ordinal + 1)
828:                 if not _record_matches_draft(successor, plan.draft):
829:                     _remove_intent(intent_path)
830:                     raise AdoptionStale(
831:                         "a different Full-verified record occupies S+1; "
832:                         "superseded adoption intent retired"
833:                     )
834:                 commit = Commit(
835:                     before=predecessor,
836:                     records=(successor,),
837:                     after=adoption_head,
838:                     durability=DurabilityReceipt(
839:                         ledger.capabilities().durability,
840:                         "reconciled from Full-verified custody; original witness unavailable",
841:                     ),
842:                 )
843:                 current = adoption_head
844:                 data["phase"] = "appended"
845:                 try:
846:                     _replace_json(intent_path, data)
847:                 except Exception as exc:
848:                     raise AdoptionCommittedIncomplete(
849:                         "adoption row is committed but appended phase recording failed",
850:                         phase="appended", head=current, commit=commit,
851:                         intent_path=intent_path, cause=exc,
852:                     ) from exc
853:         finally:
854:             _close(query)
855:             _close(ledger)
856:         return _finish(registry, plan, intent_path, data, current, commit,
857:                        "recovered", failure_hook, observed_head)

=== FULL FILE libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_initialization.py ===
1: """Fresh Arrival initialization and recovery.
2: 
3: This module is intentionally a coordinator.  It knows the Arrival contracts
4: and the registry, but it never opens a concrete store or a projection itself.
5: The file adapter is therefore just one possible implementation of the
6: operation.
7: """
8: 
9: from __future__ import annotations

132: def arrival_intent_path(target: Path | str) -> Path:
133:     """The exclusive intent sidecar for one declaration target."""
134: 
135:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
136:     return target_path.with_name(target_path.name + ".arrival-init.intent")

138: 
139: def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
140:     if isinstance(value, Path):
141:         return str(value)
142:     if isinstance(value, Profile):
143:         return value.value
144:     if isinstance(value, Head):
145:         return {
146:             "lineage": value.lineage,
147:             "ordinal": value.ordinal,
148:             "record_hash": value.record_hash,
149:         }
150:     if isinstance(value, Mapping):
151:         return {str(k): _json_value(v) for k, v in value.items()}
152:     if isinstance(value, (tuple, list)):
153:         return [_json_value(v) for v in value]
154:     return value
155: 
156: 
157: def _atomic_write(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
158:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
159:     fd, raw = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
160:     tmp = Path(raw)
161:     try:
162:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
163:             stream.write(text)
164:             stream.flush()
165:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
166:         tmp.replace(path)
167:         _fsync_parent(path)
168:     finally:
169:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
170:             tmp.unlink()
171: 
172: 
173: def _reserve(path: Path, plan: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
174:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
175:     encoded = json.dumps(_json_value(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"))
176:     flags = os.O_WRONLY | os.O_CREAT | os.O_EXCL
177:     fd = os.open(path, flags, 0o600)
178:     try:
179:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
180:             stream.write(encoded + "\n")
181:             stream.flush()
182:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
183:         _fsync_parent(path)
184:     except BaseException:
185:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
186:             path.unlink()
187:         raise
188: 
189: 
190: def _save_intent(path: Path, plan: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
191:     _atomic_write(path, json.dumps(_json_value(plan), sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n")
192: 
193: 
194: def _publish_exclusive(path: Path, text: str) -> None:
195:     """Publish a complete declaration without replacing a concurrent writer."""
196:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
197:     fd, raw = tempfile.mkstemp(prefix=f".{path.name}.", dir=path.parent)
198:     temporary = Path(raw)
199:     try:
200:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as stream:
201:             stream.write(text)
202:             stream.flush()
203:             os.fsync(stream.fileno())
204:         os.link(temporary, path)
205:         temporary.unlink()
206:         _fsync_parent(path)
207:     except BaseException:
208:         with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
209:             temporary.unlink()
210:         raise
211: 
212: 
213: def _fsync_parent(path: Path) -> None:
214:     directory_fd = os.open(path.parent, os.O_RDONLY)
215:     try:
216:         os.fsync(directory_fd)
217:     finally:
218:         os.close(directory_fd)
219: 
220: 
221: def _reserved_genesis_signer(
222:     plan: Mapping[str, Any],
223: ) -> Callable[[str, str], str | None]:
224:     """Return the exact signature reserved before the durable mint."""
225:     genesis = plan["genesis"]
226:     digest = content_commitment(
227:         genesis["kind"], genesis["at"], genesis["observer"],
228:         genesis["origin"], genesis["body"],
229:     )
230: 
231:     def sign(observer: str, offered_digest: str) -> str | None:
232:         if observer == genesis["observer"] and offered_digest == digest:
233:             return genesis["signature"]
234:         return None
235: 
236:     return sign
237: 
238: 
239: def _read_intent(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
240:     try:
241:         data = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
242:     except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
243:         raise InitializationConflict(f"cannot read initialization intent {path}: {exc}") from exc
244:     if not isinstance(data, dict) or data.get("schema") != _INTENT_SCHEMA:
245:         raise InitializationConflict(f"unsupported or malformed initialization intent: {path}")
246:     if data.get("phase") not in _PHASES:
247:         raise InitializationConflict(f"initialization intent has an invalid phase: {path}")
248:     return data

254:         "location": descriptor.location,
255:         "lineage": descriptor.lineage,
256:         "role": None if descriptor.role is None else descriptor.role.value,
257:         "query": descriptor.query,
258:         "witness": descriptor.witness,
259:     }

264:     return StoreDescriptor(
265:         backend=str(data["backend"]),
266:         location=str(data["location"]),
267:         lineage=data.get("lineage"),
268:         role=None if role is None else Profile(str(role)),
269:         query=data.get("query"),
270:         witness=data.get("witness"),
271:     )
