274: def build_declaration_anchor_draft(
275:     *,
276:     lineage: str,
277:     authored_at: float,
278:     observer: str,
279:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
280:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
281:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
282:     public_key: str | None = None,
283:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
284:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
285: ) -> RecordDraft:
286:     payload = json.dumps(
287:         {"protocol": 1, "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents]},
288:         ensure_ascii=False,
289:         separators=(",", ":"),
290:     )
291:     inner_digest = fact_commitment_hash(
292:         "_decl.genesis", authored_at, observer, "", payload
293:     )
294:     inner_signature = fact_signer(observer, inner_digest)
295:     if not inner_signature:
296:         raise ArrivalInitializationError(
297:             f"founding observer {observer!r} has no fact signer"
298:         )
299:     if fact_verify is not None and (
300:         public_key is None or not fact_verify(public_key, inner_signature, inner_digest)
301:     ):
302:         raise ArrivalInitializationError("founding fact signer does not match the public key")
303:     body = body_of_fact_row(
304:         (lineage, "_decl.genesis", authored_at, observer, "", payload, inner_signature)
305:     )
306:     outer_digest = content_commitment("fact", authored_at, observer, "", body)
307:     outer_signature = arrival_signer(observer, outer_digest)
308:     if not outer_signature:
309:         raise ArrivalInitializationError(
310:             f"founding observer {observer!r} has no Arrival signer"
311:         )
312:     if arrival_verify is not None and (
313:         public_key is None or not arrival_verify(public_key, outer_signature, outer_digest)
314:     ):
315:         raise ArrivalInitializationError("founding Arrival signer does not match the public key")
316:     return RecordDraft(
317:         kind="fact",
318:         authored_at=authored_at,
319:         observer=observer,
320:         origin="",
321:         body=body,
322:         signature=outer_signature,
323:     )
324: 
325: 
326: def _same_record(record: Mapping[str, Any], draft: RecordDraft) -> bool:
327:     return (
328:         record.get("k") == draft.kind
329:         and record.get("at") == draft.authored_at
330:         and record.get("observer") == draft.observer
331:         and record.get("origin") == draft.origin
332:         and record.get("body") == dict(draft.body)
333:         and record.get("sig") == draft.signature
334:     )
335: 
336: 
337: def _declared_key_drafts(
338:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
339:     *,
340:     observer: str,
341:     public_key: str,
342:     at: float,
343:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
344:     verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None,
345: ) -> tuple[RecordDraft, ...]:
346:     """Introduce declared observer keys using the valid founding key."""
347:     keys: dict[str, str] = {}
348:     for document in documents:
349:         if document["kind"] != "_decl.observer-defined":
350:             continue
351:         key = document["payload"].get("key")
352:         if key is None:
353:             continue
354:         subject = document["subject"]
355:         try:
356:             if not isinstance(key, str) or len(base64.b64decode(key, validate=True)) != 32:
357:                 raise ValueError
358:         except (ValueError, TypeError):
359:             raise InitializationConflict(
360:                 f"observer {subject!r} has a malformed public key"
361:             ) from None
362:         if subject == observer:
363:             if key != public_key:
364:                 raise InitializationConflict(
365:                     "declared founding key differs from genesis public key"
366:                 )
367:             continue
368:         if subject in keys and keys[subject] != key:
369:             raise InitializationConflict(f"observer {subject!r} has conflicting declared keys")
370:         keys[subject] = key
371:     drafts: list[RecordDraft] = []
372:     for subject, key in keys.items():
373:         body = {"observer": subject, "key": key}
374:         digest = content_commitment("key", at, observer, "", body)
375:         signature = signer(observer, digest)
376:         if not signature or (
377:             verify is not None and not verify(public_key, signature, digest)
378:         ):
379:             raise InitializationConflict("founding signer cannot authenticate key introduction")
380:         drafts.append(RecordDraft("key", at, observer, "", body, signature))
381:     return tuple(drafts)
382: 
383: 
384: def _draft_dict(draft: RecordDraft) -> dict[str, Any]:
385:     return {
386:         "kind": draft.kind,
387:         "authored_at": draft.authored_at,
388:         "observer": draft.observer,
389:         "origin": draft.origin,
390:         "body": dict(draft.body),
391:         "signature": draft.signature,
392:     }
393: 
394: 
395: def _bootstrap_matches(ledger: Any, current: Head, drafts: Sequence[RecordDraft]) -> bool:
396:     return current.ordinal >= len(drafts) and all(
397:         _same_record(ledger.read(ordinal), draft)
398:         for ordinal, draft in enumerate(drafts, start=1)
399:     )
400: 
401: 
402: def _phase(plan: dict[str, Any], value: str) -> None:
403:     plan["phase"] = value
404: 
405: 
406: def _check_preconditions(
407:     target: Path,
408:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
409:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
410:     declaration_text: str,
411:     observer: str,
412:     public_key: str,
413:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
414:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
415:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
416: ) -> None:
417:     if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
418:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires an Authority descriptor")
419:     if not observer or not isinstance(observer, str):
420:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires a non-empty observer")
421:     if not isinstance(public_key, str) or not public_key:
422:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires a founding public key")
423:     try:
424:         if len(base64.b64decode(public_key, validate=True)) != 32:
425:             raise ValueError
426:     except (ValueError, TypeError):
427:         raise InitializationConflict(
428:             "founding public key must be raw-32-byte base64"
429:         ) from None
430:     if not isinstance(declaration_text, str) or not declaration_text.strip():
431:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration text must be non-empty UTF-8 text")
432:     if not callable(signer) or not callable(fact_signer) or not callable(arrival_signer):
433:         raise InitializationConflict("fresh initialization requires signer callables")
434:     if not isinstance(documents, Sequence) or isinstance(documents, (str, bytes)):
435:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration documents must be a sequence")
436:     try:
437:         json.dumps([dict(document) for document in documents], ensure_ascii=False)
438:     except (TypeError, ValueError) as exc:
439:         raise InitializationConflict(f"declaration documents are not JSON-safe: {exc}") from exc
440:     if target.exists():
441:         raise InitializationConflict(f"vertex file already exists: {target}")
442: 
443: 
444: def _validate_declaration(
445:     target: Path,
446:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
447:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
448:     declaration_text: str,
449:     lineage: str,
450: ) -> None:
451:     """Check the signed declaration inputs before reserving or minting.
452: 
453:     The declaration text is the human ingress form and the document sequence
454:     is what gets committed into ``_decl.genesis``.  They must describe the
455:     same residence and identity, otherwise recovery could faithfully preserve
456:     a plan that never represented the requested target.
457:     """
458:     try:
459:         from lang import parse_vertex, validate_vertex, vertex_to_documents
460: 
461:         ast = parse_vertex(declaration_text, path=target)
462:         validate_vertex(ast)
463:     except Exception as exc:
464:         raise InitializationConflict(
465:             f"declaration text is not valid Vertex grammar: {exc}"
466:         ) from exc
467:     from .declaration import validate_arrival_runtime_identity
468: 
469:     validate_arrival_runtime_identity(
470:         ast.name,
471:         ast.loops,
472:         refusal=InitializationConflict,
473:     )
474:     parsed_documents = [document.as_json() for document in vertex_to_documents(ast)]
475:     if [dict(document) for document in documents] != parsed_documents:
476:         raise InitializationConflict(
477:             "declaration text and declaration documents disagree"
478:         )
479:     backend = ast.store_backend
480:     if (
481:         backend is None
482:         or backend.name != descriptor.backend
483:         or backend.lineage != lineage
484:         or backend.role != Profile.AUTHORITY.value
485:     ):
486:         raise InitializationConflict(
487:             "declaration residence must match the requested backend, lineage and authority role"
488:         )
489:     location = ast.store_location
490:     if descriptor.backend == "file" and ast.store is not None:
491:         location = str(ast.store)
492:     if location is None:
493:         raise InitializationConflict("declaration residence must include a store location")
494:     if descriptor.backend == "file":
495:         parsed_location = (target.parent / location).resolve()
496:         requested_location = Path(descriptor.location).resolve()
497:         locations_match = parsed_location == requested_location
498:     else:
499:         # DSNs are adapter-owned and must survive the engine untouched.
500:         locations_match = location == descriptor.location
501:     if not locations_match:
502:         raise InitializationConflict(
503:             "declaration residence location does not match the requested descriptor"
504:         )
505: 
506: 
507: def initialize_arrival(
508:     registry: BackendRegistry,
509:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
510:     *,
511:     target: Path | str,
512:     documents: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]],
513:     declaration_text: str,
514:     observer: str,
515:     public_key: str,
516:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
517:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
518:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None],
519:     fact_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
520:     arrival_verify: Callable[[str, str, str], bool] | None = None,
521:     lineage: str | None = None,
522:     authored_at: float | None = None,
523:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
524: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
525:     """Mint and publish a fresh Arrival declaration with recoverable intent."""
526:     target_path = Path(target).resolve()
527:     intent = arrival_intent_path(target_path)
528:     _check_preconditions(
529:         target_path, descriptor, documents, declaration_text, observer, public_key,
530:         signer, fact_signer, arrival_signer,
531:     )
532:     if intent.exists():
533:         raise InitializationRecoveryRequired(
534:             f"unfinished initialization intent exists: {intent}; recover explicitly"
535:         )
536:     chosen_lineage = lineage or descriptor.lineage or new_lineage()
537:     if descriptor.lineage is not None and descriptor.lineage != chosen_lineage:
538:         raise InitializationConflict("descriptor lineage does not match reserved lineage")
539:     _validate_declaration(
540:         target_path, descriptor, documents, declaration_text, chosen_lineage
541:     )
542:     at = time.time() if authored_at is None else authored_at
543:     genesis_body = {
544:         "protocol": 1,
545:         "lineage": chosen_lineage,
546:         "key": public_key,
547:     }
548:     genesis_digest = content_commitment(
549:         "genesis", at, observer, "", genesis_body
550:     )
551:     genesis_signature = signer(observer, genesis_digest)
552:     if not genesis_signature:
553:         raise InitializationConflict("founding signer produced no genesis signature")
554:     if arrival_verify is not None and not arrival_verify(
555:         public_key, genesis_signature, genesis_digest
556:     ):
557:         raise InitializationConflict("founding genesis signer does not match the public key")
558:     draft = build_declaration_anchor_draft(
559:         lineage=chosen_lineage,
560:         authored_at=at,
561:         observer=observer,
562:         documents=documents,
563:         fact_signer=fact_signer,
564:         arrival_signer=arrival_signer,
565:         public_key=public_key,
566:         fact_verify=fact_verify,
567:         arrival_verify=arrival_verify,
568:     )
569:     key_drafts = _declared_key_drafts(
570:         documents, observer=observer, public_key=public_key, at=at,
571:         signer=arrival_signer, verify=arrival_verify,
572:     )
573:     if key_drafts:
574:         # The minimum backend contract supports one record. Additional keys
575:         # and declaration genesis form one larger atomic unit; refuse its
576:         # unsupported size before reserving an intent or minting a lineage.
577:         ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
578:         try:
579:             limit = ledger.capabilities().max_atomic_records
580:             if limit is not None and len(key_drafts) + 1 > limit:
581:                 raise AtomicLimitExceeded(
582:                     f"initialization needs {len(key_drafts) + 1} records; atomic limit is {limit}"
583:                 )
584:         finally:
585:             for resource in (query, ledger):
586:                 close = getattr(resource, "close", None)
587:                 if callable(close):
588:                     close()
589:     planned = {
590:         "schema": _INTENT_SCHEMA,
591:         "phase": "reserved",
592:         "target": str(target_path),
593:         "descriptor": _descriptor_dict(
594:             StoreDescriptor(
595:                 descriptor.backend, descriptor.location, chosen_lineage,
596:                 descriptor.role, descriptor.query, descriptor.witness,
597:             )
598:         ),
599:         "lineage": chosen_lineage,
600:         "observer": observer,
601:         "public_key": public_key,
602:         "at": at,
603:         "genesis": {
604:             "kind": "genesis",
605:             "at": at,
606:             "observer": observer,
607:             "origin": "",
608:             "body": genesis_body,
609:             "signature": genesis_signature,
610:         },
611:         "documents": [dict(document) for document in documents],
612:         "declaration_text": declaration_text,
613:         "draft": _draft_dict(draft),
614:         "key_drafts": [_draft_dict(key_draft) for key_draft in key_drafts],
615:     }
616:     _reserve(intent, planned)
617:     try:
618:         return _continue(
619:             registry, intent, planned, failure_hook=failure_hook,
620:         )
621:     except BaseException:
622:         # The sidecar is the recovery record.  It is removed only once the
623:         # declaration bytes have been atomically published.
624:         raise
625: 
626: 
627: def _continue(
628:     registry: BackendRegistry,
629:     intent: Path,
630:     plan: dict[str, Any],
631:     *,
632:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None,
633: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
634:     descriptor = _descriptor_from(plan["descriptor"])
635:     target = Path(plan["target"])
636:     draft_data = plan["draft"]
637:     draft = RecordDraft(
638:         kind=draft_data["kind"], authored_at=draft_data["authored_at"],
639:         observer=draft_data["observer"], origin=draft_data["origin"],
640:         body=draft_data["body"], signature=draft_data["signature"],
641:     )
642:     drafts = tuple(RecordDraft(**data) for data in plan.get("key_drafts", ())) + (draft,)
643:     ledger = query = None
644:     head: Head | None = None
645:     commit = None
646:     try:
647:         if plan["phase"] == "reserved":
648:             ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
649:             # PreGenesis carries no absence claim.  The adapter's exclusive
650:             # mint is the authority for an absent, existing, or corrupt
651:             # location and refuses safely in the latter two cases.
652:             try:
653:                 head = ledger.mint({
654:                     "observer": plan["observer"],
655:                     "signer": _reserved_genesis_signer(plan),
656:                     "key": plan["public_key"], "lineage": plan["lineage"],
657:                     "origin": "", "at": plan["at"],
658:                 })
659:             except Exception as mint_error:
660:                 from .arrival import GenesisRefused
661:                 from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
662:                 from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
663: 
664:                 if isinstance(mint_error, NotWitnessed):
665:                     raise InitializationUnwitnessed(
666:                         "genesis committed but its witness is unknown; recover "
667:                         "the initialization intent before retrying",
668:                         phase="reserved", lineage=plan["lineage"],
669:                         fact_id=plan["lineage"], head=mint_error.head,
670:                         commit=mint_error.commit, intent_path=intent,
671:                         cause=mint_error,
672:                     ) from mint_error
673:                 # A prior mint may have committed while its witness was
674:                 # unknown.  The failed exclusive mint is the proof that this
675:                 # is not a fresh location; only an exact custody comparison
676:                 # can turn that case into recoverable progress.
677:                 try:
678:                     head = _head_and_check_genesis(registry, descriptor, plan)
679:                 except Exception:
680:                     if isinstance(mint_error, (ContractRefusal, GenesisRefused)):
681:                         raise mint_error from None
682:                     raise InitializationOutcomeUnknown(
683:                         "genesis mint outcome is unknown; recover the durable "
684:                         "initialization intent before retrying",
685:                         phase="reserved", lineage=plan["lineage"],
686:                         fact_id=plan["lineage"], captured_head=None,
687:                         intent_path=intent, cause=mint_error,
688:                     ) from mint_error
689:                 if head.ordinal != 0:
690:                     raise mint_error from None
691:             _phase(plan, "minted")
692:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
693:             if failure_hook:
694:                 failure_hook("after-mint")
695:         else:
696:             head = _head_and_check_genesis(registry, descriptor, plan)
697:         if plan["phase"] == "minted":
698:             if ledger is None:
699:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
700:             current = ledger.head()
701:             if current.ordinal == 0:
702:                 try:
703:                     commit = ledger.append(current, drafts)
704:                 except Exception as exc:
705:                     # Contract/grammar refusals are precommit diagnostics;
706:                     # an adapter exception outside those public families did
707:                     # not establish whether its append landed.
708:                     from .arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
709: 
710:                     if isinstance(exc, ContractRefusal):
711:                         raise
712:                     from .arrival import GenesisRefused
713: 
714:                     if isinstance(exc, GenesisRefused):
715:                         raise
716:                     from .arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
717: 
718:                     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
719:                         raise InitializationUnwitnessed(
720:                             "declaration committed but its witness is unknown; "
721:                             "recover the initialization intent before retrying",
722:                             phase="minted", lineage=plan["lineage"],
723:                             fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), head=exc.head,
724:                             commit=exc.commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
725:                         ) from exc
726:                     raise InitializationOutcomeUnknown(
727:                         "declaration append outcome is unknown; recover the "
728:                         "initialization intent before retrying",
729:                         phase="minted", lineage=plan["lineage"],
730:                         fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), captured_head=current,
731:                         intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
732:                     ) from exc
733:                 head = commit.after
734:             elif _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
735:                 head = current
736:             else:
737:                 raise InitializationConflict("ledger contains a conflicting post-genesis record")
738:             _phase(plan, "declared")
739:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
740:             if failure_hook:
741:                 failure_hook("after-declaration")
742:         if plan["phase"] == "declared":
743:             if ledger is None:
744:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
745:             current = ledger.head()
746:             # The declaration append is already durable at this point.  Keep
747:             # its observed head while projection sync runs so a sync failure
748:             # is reported as committed-incomplete with concrete identity.
749:             head = current
750:             if not _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
751:                 raise InitializationConflict(
752:                     "ledger no longer contains the planned declaration genesis"
753:                 )
754:             result = sync_projection(registry, descriptor)
755:             head = result.captured_head
756:             _phase(plan, "synced")
757:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
758:             if failure_hook:
759:                 failure_hook("after-sync")
760:         elif plan["phase"] in {"synced", "published"}:
761:             if ledger is None:
762:                 ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
763:             current = ledger.head()
764:             # Recovery has an observed committed head before attempting
765:             # publication or intent cleanup.
766:             head = current
767:             if not _bootstrap_matches(ledger, current, drafts):
768:                 raise InitializationConflict(
769:                     "ledger no longer contains the planned declaration genesis"
770:                 )
771:         else:
772:             head = ledger.head() if ledger is not None else _head_and_check_genesis(
773:                 registry, descriptor, plan
774:             )
775:         if plan["phase"] == "synced":
776:             if target.exists():
777:                 existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
778:                 if existing != plan["declaration_text"]:
779:                     raise InitializationConflict(
780:                         "existing declaration differs from initialization intent"
781:                     )
782:             else:
783:                 try:
784:                     _publish_exclusive(target, plan["declaration_text"])
785:                 except FileExistsError:
786:                     existing = target.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
787:                     if existing != plan["declaration_text"]:
788:                         raise InitializationConflict(
789:                             "a concurrent declaration differs from initialization intent"
790:                         ) from None
791:             _phase(plan, "published")
792:             _save_intent(intent, plan)
793:             if failure_hook:
794:                 failure_hook("after-publication")
795:         if plan["phase"] == "published":
796:             with suppress(FileNotFoundError):
797:                 intent.unlink()
798:                 _fsync_parent(intent)
799:         return ArrivalInitializationResult(
800:             target_path=str(target), location=descriptor.location,
801:             lineage=plan["lineage"], head=head,
802:             phase=plan["phase"], file_written=target.exists(),
803:         )
804:     except Exception as exc:
805:         if isinstance(
806:             exc,
807:             (
808:                 InitializationOutcomeUnknown,
809:                 InitializationUnwitnessed,
810:                 InitializationCommittedIncomplete,
811:             ),
812:         ):
813:             raise
814:         if head is not None and plan["phase"] in {"minted", "declared", "synced", "published"}:
815:             raise InitializationCommittedIncomplete(
816:                 "initialization committed but a later phase failed; recover "
817:                 "the durable intent",
818:                 phase=plan["phase"], lineage=plan["lineage"],
819:                 fact_id=str(draft.body["id"]), captured_head=head,
820:                 commit=commit, intent_path=intent, cause=exc,
821:             ) from exc
822:         raise
823:     finally:
824:         close = getattr(query, "close", None)
825:         if callable(close):
826:             close()
827:         close = getattr(ledger, "close", None)
828:         if callable(close):
829:             close()
830: 
831: 
832: def _head_and_check_genesis(
833:     registry: BackendRegistry,
834:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
835:     plan: Mapping[str, Any],
836: ) -> Head:
837:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
838:     try:
839:         head = ledger.head()
840:         record = ledger.read(0)
841:         body = record.get("body")
842:         genesis = plan["genesis"]
843:         if (
844:             record.get("k") != genesis["kind"]
845:             or record.get("at") != genesis["at"]
846:             or record.get("observer") != genesis["observer"]
847:             or record.get("origin") != genesis["origin"]
848:             or body != genesis["body"]
849:             or record.get("sig") != genesis["signature"]
850:         ):
851:             raise InitializationConflict("existing genesis differs from initialization intent")
852:         if head.lineage != plan["lineage"]:
853:             raise InitializationConflict(
854:                 "existing ledger lineage differs from initialization intent"
855:             )
856:         return head
857:     finally:
858:         getattr(query, "close", lambda: None)()
859:         getattr(ledger, "close", lambda: None)()
860: 
861: 
862: def recover_arrival_initialization(
863:     registry: BackendRegistry,
864:     intent: Path | str,
865:     *,
866:     # Retained as ignored compatibility parameters for callers of the first
867:     # recovery prototype. Recovery signs nothing: it uses the exact reserved
868:     # genesis signature from the durable intent.
869:     signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
870:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
871:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None,
872:     failure_hook: Callable[[str], None] | None = None,
873: ) -> ArrivalInitializationResult:
874:     """Resume an intent by comparing exact custody content at every phase."""
875:     intent_path = Path(intent).resolve()
876:     if not intent_path.exists():
877:         raise InitializationConflict(f"initialization intent does not exist: {intent_path}")
878:     plan = _read_intent(intent_path)
879:     return _continue(
880:         registry, intent_path, plan, failure_hook=failure_hook,
881:     )

=== FULL FILE libs/engine/src/engine/credentials.py ===
1: """Operation-fresh signing credentials for engine write coordinators.
2: 
3: This module contains values only.  It has no store, projection, or custody
4: dependency, so supported Arrival coordinators can accept signer injection
5: without importing the legacy SQLite handle implementation.
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: from collections.abc import Callable
11: from dataclasses import dataclass
12: from enum import StrEnum
13: from pathlib import Path
14: from typing import Protocol
15: 
16: from .arrival_contract import Head
17: 
18: __all__ = [
19:     "BindingResolver",
20:     "CredentialBindingEvidence",
21:     "CredentialBindingRefused",
22:     "CapturedSigningContext",
23:     "CredentialProvider",
24:     "CredentialPurpose",
25:     "CredentialRequest",
26:     "ResolvedCredential",
27:     "SignatureVerifier",
28:     "SigningDomain",
29:     "WriteCredentials",
30: ]
31: 
32: 
33: class SigningDomain(StrEnum):
34:     """The protocol domain whose commitment is being signed."""
35: 
36:     FACT = "fact"
37:     ARRIVAL = "arrival"
38:     TICK = "tick"
39: 
40: 
41: class CredentialPurpose(StrEnum):
42:     """Why one operation needs a signing credential."""
43: 
44:     AUTHORSHIP = "authorship"
45:     KEY_INTRODUCTION = "key-introduction"
46:     RECEIPT = "receipt"
47:     INITIALIZATION = "initialization"
48: 
49: 
50: @dataclass(frozen=True)
51: class CredentialRequest:
52:     """An exact local namespace, protocol observer, domain and purpose."""
53: 
54:     namespace: str
55:     observer: str
56:     domain: SigningDomain
57:     purpose: CredentialPurpose
58: 
59:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
60:         if not isinstance(self.namespace, str) or not self.namespace:
61:             raise ValueError("credential namespace must be an exact nonempty string")
62:         if not isinstance(self.observer, str) or not self.observer:
63:             raise ValueError("credential observer must be an exact nonempty string")
64:         if not isinstance(self.domain, SigningDomain):
65:             raise ValueError("credential domain must be a SigningDomain")
66:         if not isinstance(self.purpose, CredentialPurpose):
67:             raise ValueError("credential purpose must be a CredentialPurpose")
68: 
69: 
70: @dataclass(frozen=True)
71: class CredentialBindingEvidence:
72:     """Serializable public evidence for one resolved local binding."""
73: 
74:     request: CredentialRequest
75:     key_ref: str
76:     algorithm: str
77:     public_key: str
78:     provenance: str
79: 
80: 
81: @dataclass(frozen=True)
82: class ResolvedCredential:
83:     """Public binding evidence paired with its private signing operation."""
84: 
85:     evidence: CredentialBindingEvidence
86:     sign_digest: Callable[[str], str]
87: 
88: 
89: BindingResolver = Callable[[CredentialRequest], ResolvedCredential | None]
90: SignatureVerifier = Callable[[SigningDomain, str, str, str], bool]
91: 
92: 
93: class CredentialBindingRefused(ValueError):
94:     """A mapped binding could not safely authorize one planned signature."""
95: 
96:     def __init__(
97:         self,
98:         reason: str,
99:         *,
100:         request: CredentialRequest | None = None,
101:         captured_head: Head | None = None,
102:         use_position: int | None = None,
103:         evidence: CredentialBindingEvidence | None = None,
104:     ) -> None:
105:         super().__init__(reason)
106:         self.reason = reason
107:         self.request = request
108:         self.captured_head = captured_head
109:         self.use_position = use_position
110:         self.evidence = evidence
111: 
112: 
113: @dataclass(frozen=True)
114: class CapturedSigningContext:
115:     """Immutable authorization evidence derived from one verified prefix."""
116: 
117:     captured_head: Head
118:     author_keys: tuple[tuple[str, tuple[str, ...]], ...]
119:     receipt_keys: tuple[str, ...]
120: 
121:     @property
122:     def use_position(self) -> int:
123:         return self.captured_head.ordinal + 1
124: 
125:     def keys_for_author(self, observer: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
126:         return next(
127:             (keys for name, keys in self.author_keys if name == observer),
128:             (),
129:         )
130: 
131: 
132: class CredentialResolutionSession:
133:     """Operation-local mapped resolution with cross-domain binding coherence."""
134: 
135:     __slots__ = ("_bindings", "_evidence")
136: 
137:     def __init__(self) -> None:
138:         self._bindings: dict[tuple[str, str], tuple[str, str]] = {}
139:         self._evidence: list[CredentialBindingEvidence] = []
140: 
141:     @property
142:     def evidence(self) -> tuple[CredentialBindingEvidence, ...]:
143:         return tuple(self._evidence)
144: 
145:     def sign(
146:         self,
147:         credentials: WriteCredentials,
148:         request: CredentialRequest,
149:         digest: str,
150:         *,
151:         context: CapturedSigningContext,
152:         authorized_keys: tuple[str, ...],
153:         required: bool,
154:     ) -> str | None:
155:         """Resolve, authorize and independently verify one mapped signature."""
156:         resolver = credentials.binding_resolver
157:         verifier = credentials.signature_verifier
158:         if not credentials.mapped or resolver is None or verifier is None:
159:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
160:                 "mapped signing requires a complete credential configuration",
161:                 request=request,
162:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
163:                 use_position=context.use_position,
164:             )
165:         try:
166:             resolved = resolver(request)
167:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
168:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
169:                 exc.reason,
170:                 request=request,
171:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
172:                 use_position=context.use_position,
173:                 evidence=exc.evidence,
174:             ) from exc
175:         except Exception as exc:
176:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
177:                 "credential resolver failed",
178:                 request=request,
179:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
180:                 use_position=context.use_position,
181:             ) from exc
182:         if resolved is None:
183:             if not required:
184:                 return None
185:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
186:                 "missing-required",
187:                 request=request,
188:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
189:                 use_position=context.use_position,
190:             )
191:         if not isinstance(resolved, ResolvedCredential):
192:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
193:                 "resolver returned an invalid credential value",
194:                 request=request,
195:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
196:                 use_position=context.use_position,
197:             )
198:         evidence = resolved.evidence
199:         if (
200:             not isinstance(evidence, CredentialBindingEvidence)
201:             or evidence.request != request
202:             or not all(
203:                 isinstance(value, str) and bool(value)
204:                 for value in (
205:                     evidence.key_ref,
206:                     evidence.algorithm,
207:                     evidence.public_key,
208:                     evidence.provenance,
209:                 )
210:             )
211:             or not callable(resolved.sign_digest)
212:         ):
213:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
214:                 "resolver returned contradictory binding evidence",
215:                 request=request,
216:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
217:                 use_position=context.use_position,
218:                 evidence=evidence
219:                 if isinstance(evidence, CredentialBindingEvidence)
220:                 else None,
221:             )
222:         if evidence.public_key not in authorized_keys:
223:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
224:                 "resolved public key is not authorized at the captured position",
225:                 request=request,
226:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
227:                 use_position=context.use_position,
228:                 evidence=evidence,
229:             )
230:         binding_key = (request.namespace, request.observer)
231:         binding_value = (evidence.key_ref, evidence.public_key)
232:         prior = self._bindings.get(binding_key)
233:         if prior is not None and prior != binding_value:
234:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
235:                 "one mapped observer resolved to different bindings across domains",
236:                 request=request,
237:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
238:                 use_position=context.use_position,
239:                 evidence=evidence,
240:             )
241:         try:
242:             signature = resolved.sign_digest(digest)
243:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
244:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
245:                 exc.reason,
246:                 request=request,
247:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
248:                 use_position=context.use_position,
249:                 evidence=exc.evidence or evidence,
250:             ) from exc
251:         except Exception as exc:
252:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
253:                 "credential signer failed",
254:                 request=request,
255:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
256:                 use_position=context.use_position,
257:                 evidence=evidence,
258:             ) from exc
259:         if not isinstance(signature, str) or not signature:
260:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
261:                 "mapped signer returned no signature",
262:                 request=request,
263:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
264:                 use_position=context.use_position,
265:                 evidence=evidence,
266:             )
267:         try:
268:             verified = verifier(
269:                 request.domain, evidence.public_key, signature, digest
270:             )
271:         except CredentialBindingRefused as exc:
272:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
273:                 exc.reason,
274:                 request=request,
275:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
276:                 use_position=context.use_position,
277:                 evidence=exc.evidence or evidence,
278:             ) from exc
279:         except Exception as exc:
280:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
281:                 "credential signature verifier failed",
282:                 request=request,
283:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
284:                 use_position=context.use_position,
285:                 evidence=evidence,
286:             ) from exc
287:         if not verified:
288:             raise CredentialBindingRefused(
289:                 "mapped signature did not verify under its resolved public key",
290:                 request=request,
291:                 captured_head=context.captured_head,
292:                 use_position=context.use_position,
293:                 evidence=evidence,
294:             )
295:         self._bindings[binding_key] = binding_value
296:         self._evidence.append(evidence)
297:         return signature
298: 
299: 
300: @dataclass(frozen=True)
301: class WriteCredentials:
302:     """The distinct domain-separated signers supplied for one write.
303: 
304:     ``tick_signer`` signs tick commitments, ``fact_signer`` signs inner fact
305:     commitments, and ``arrival_signer`` signs outer Arrival envelopes.  A
306:     missing signer means that axis is unsigned; one signer is never used as a
307:     fallback for another.
308:     """
309: 
310:     tick_signer: Callable[[str], str] | None = None
311:     fact_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None
312:     arrival_signer: Callable[[str, str], str | None] | None = None
313:     binding_namespace: str | None = None
314:     receipt_observer: str | None = None
315:     binding_resolver: BindingResolver | None = None
316:     signature_verifier: SignatureVerifier | None = None
317: 
318:     def __post_init__(self) -> None:
319:         mapped_parts = (
320:             self.binding_namespace,
321:             self.binding_resolver,
322:             self.signature_verifier,
323:         )
324:         mapped = all(part is not None for part in mapped_parts)
325:         if any(part is not None for part in mapped_parts) and not mapped:
326:             raise ValueError(
327:                 "mapped credentials require namespace, resolver, and verifier together"
328:             )
329:         if self.receipt_observer is not None and not mapped:
330:             raise ValueError("receipt observer requires mapped credentials")
331:         if mapped and any(
332:             signer is not None
333:             for signer in (self.tick_signer, self.fact_signer, self.arrival_signer)
334:         ):
335:             raise ValueError("mapped credentials cannot include legacy signer callbacks")
336:         if self.binding_resolver is not None and not callable(self.binding_resolver):
337:             raise ValueError("credential binding resolver must be callable")
338:         if self.signature_verifier is not None and not callable(self.signature_verifier):
339:             raise ValueError("credential signature verifier must be callable")
340:         if self.binding_namespace is not None and (
341:             not isinstance(self.binding_namespace, str) or not self.binding_namespace
342:         ):
343:             raise ValueError("credential namespace must be an exact nonempty string")
344:         if self.receipt_observer is not None and (
345:             not isinstance(self.receipt_observer, str) or not self.receipt_observer
346:         ):
347:             raise ValueError("receipt observer must be an exact nonempty string")
348: 
349:     @property
350:     def mapped(self) -> bool:
351:         """Whether signing must use explicit mapped binding resolution."""
352:         return self.binding_namespace is not None
353: 
354: 
355: class CredentialProvider(Protocol):
356:     """Supply credentials at the moment of each write operation."""
357: 
358:     def for_write(self, vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials: ...

=== FULL FILE libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_maintenance.py ===
1: """Explicit, attested maintenance of Arrival query projections.
2: 
3: Reads never import this module.  The public coordinator first earns the normal
4: registry/head-attestation result, then obtains a private adapter maintenance
5: handle and asks it to advance derived state through one verified full head.
6: """
7: 
8: from __future__ import annotations
9: 
10: from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
11: from contextlib import suppress
12: from dataclasses import dataclass
13: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Protocol, runtime_checkable
14: 
15: from .arrival_contract import (
16:     Full,
17:     Head,
18:     HeadMismatch,
19:     NotAuthority,
20:     NotSupported,
21:     Profile,
22:     ProjectionAbsent,
23:     ProjectionRequirement,
24:     QuerySnapshot,
25:     Watermark,
26: )
27: from .arrival_head_seam import AttestedLedger, Compared, Indeterminate, PreGenesis
28: 
29: if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover - annotations only
30:     from .arrival_contract import ArrivalQuery, StoreDescriptor
31:     from .arrival_registry import BackendRegistry
32: 
33: __all__ = [
34:     "MaintenanceAdvance",
35:     "MaintenanceCapabilities",
36:     "MaintenanceOpener",
37:     "ProjectionMaintenance",
38:     "ProjectionSyncError",
39:     "ProjectionSyncResult",
40:     "preflight_projection_maintenance",
41:     "sync_projection",
42: ]
43: 
44: 
45: @dataclass(frozen=True)
46: class MaintenanceCapabilities:
47:     """The derived-state operations an adapter maintenance handle supports."""
48: 
49:     catch_up: bool
50:     rebuild: bool = False
51: 
52: 
53: @dataclass(frozen=True)
54: class MaintenanceAdvance:
55:     """Adapter-local watermarks observed under its maintenance lock."""
56: 
57:     before: Watermark | None
58:     after: Watermark
59:     changed: bool
60: 
61: 
62: @runtime_checkable
63: class ProjectionMaintenance(Protocol):
64:     """A narrow derived-state authority, separate from query and custody."""
65: 
66:     def capabilities(self) -> MaintenanceCapabilities: ...
67: 
68:     def catch_up(self, through: Head) -> MaintenanceAdvance:
69:         """Advance through ``through`` transactionally, never past it."""
70:         ...
71: 
72:     def close(self) -> None: ...
73: 
74: 
75: MaintenanceOpener = Callable[["StoreDescriptor"], ProjectionMaintenance]
76: 
77: 
78: @dataclass(frozen=True)
79: class ProjectionSyncResult:
80:     """The attested head and derived prefix established by one explicit sync."""
81: 
82:     captured_head: Head
83:     target: Head
84:     projected_before: Head | None
85:     projected_after: Head
86:     view_generation: str | None
87:     changed: bool
88:     rebuilt: bool = False
89: 
90: 
91: class ProjectionSyncError(Exception):
92:     """Maintenance failed after derived mutation may have been attempted.
93: 
94:     ``observed_after`` is custody-completed evidence read after the failure.
95:     ``None`` means that the coordinator could not establish a resulting
96:     projection prefix; it does not claim that no derived write occurred.
97:     """
98: 
99:     def __init__(
100:         self,
101:         message: str,
102:         *,
103:         target: Head,
104:         projected_before: Head | None,
105:         observed_after: Head | None,
106:         cause: BaseException,
107:         coordinator_phase: str | None = None,
108:         effects: Mapping[str, Mapping[str, str]] | None = None,
109:     ) -> None:
110:         super().__init__(message)
111:         self.target = target
112:         self.projected_before = projected_before
113:         self.observed_after = observed_after
114:         self.cause = cause
115:         self.coordinator_phase = coordinator_phase
116:         self.effects = effects
117: 
118: 
119: def _close_quietly(handle: object) -> None:
120:     close = getattr(handle, "close", None)
121:     if callable(close):
122:         with suppress(Exception):
123:             close()
124: 
125: 
126: def _captured_head(ledger: AttestedLedger) -> Head:
127:     comparison = ledger.opened.comparison
128:     if isinstance(comparison, PreGenesis):
129:         if comparison.ledger_refusal is not None:
130:             raise comparison.ledger_refusal
131:         raise NotSupported("a pre-genesis ledger has no projection target")
132:     if isinstance(comparison, Indeterminate):
133:         raise comparison.refusal
134:     if not isinstance(comparison, Compared):
135:         raise TypeError(f"unsupported head comparison {comparison!r}")
136:     return comparison.presented
137: 
138: 
139: def _resolve_head(ledger: AttestedLedger, watermark: Watermark | None) -> Head | None:
140:     if watermark is None:
141:         return None
142:     return ledger.head_at(watermark)
143: 
144: 
145: def _snapshot_state(
146:     query: ArrivalQuery, ledger: AttestedLedger, *, bound: Head
147: ) -> tuple[Head | None, str | None]:
148:     snapshot: QuerySnapshot | None = None
149:     try:
150:         try:
151:             snapshot = query.open_snapshot(
152:                 captured_head=bound,
153:                 requirement=ProjectionRequirement.ALLOW_BEHIND,
154:             )
155:         except ProjectionAbsent:
156:             return None, None
157:         return _resolve_head(ledger, snapshot.represented), snapshot.view_generation
158:     finally:
159:         if snapshot is not None:
160:             _close_quietly(snapshot)
161: 
162: 
163: def _verify_target(ledger: AttestedLedger, captured: Head, target: Head) -> Head:
164:     if target.lineage != captured.lineage:
165:         raise NotAuthority(
166:             f"maintenance target names lineage {target.lineage}, but the "
167:             f"attested ledger presents {captured.lineage}"
168:         )
169:     if target.ordinal > captured.ordinal:
170:         raise HeadMismatch(
171:             f"maintenance target ordinal {target.ordinal} exceeds the attested "
172:             f"captured ordinal {captured.ordinal}"
173:         )
174:     resolved = ledger.head_at(Watermark(target.lineage, target.ordinal))
175:     if resolved != target:
176:         raise HeadMismatch("maintenance target no longer resolves to its recorded hash")
177:     verified = ledger.verify(Full(through=resolved))
178:     if verified != resolved:
179:         raise HeadMismatch("full verification returned a head other than its target")
180:     return verified
181: 
182: 
183: def _require_catch_up(
184:     registry: BackendRegistry, descriptor: StoreDescriptor, *, rebuild: bool = False
185: ) -> ProjectionMaintenance:
186:     maintenance = registry._maintenance_for(descriptor)
187:     try:
188:         capabilities = maintenance.capabilities()
189:         if rebuild or not capabilities.catch_up:
190:             raise NotSupported(
191:                 "projection rebuild is not supported"
192:                 if rebuild
193:                 else f"backend {descriptor.backend!r} does not support projection catch-up"
194:             )
195:         if capabilities.rebuild:
196:             raise TypeError(
197:                 "first-stage maintenance providers must advertise rebuild=False"
198:             )
199:         return maintenance
200:     except BaseException:
201:         _close_quietly(maintenance)
202:         raise
203: 
204: 
205: def preflight_projection_maintenance(
206:     registry: BackendRegistry, descriptor: StoreDescriptor, *, through: Head
207: ) -> None:
208:     """Prove catch-up availability at an attested head without mutating derived state."""
209:     if descriptor.role not in (Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA):
210:         raise NotAuthority(
211:             "projection maintenance requires an explicit Authority or Replica role"
212:         )
213:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
214:     maintenance: ProjectionMaintenance | None = None
215:     try:
216:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
217:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
218:         captured = _captured_head(ledger)
219:         if captured != through:
220:             raise HeadMismatch("projection preflight head moved after adoption capture")
221:         _verify_target(ledger, captured, through)
222:         maintenance = _require_catch_up(registry, descriptor)
223:     finally:
224:         if maintenance is not None:
225:             _close_quietly(maintenance)
226:         _close_quietly(query)
227:         _close_quietly(ledger)
228: 
229: 
230: def sync_projection(
231:     registry: BackendRegistry,
232:     descriptor: StoreDescriptor,
233:     *,
234:     through: Head | None = None,
235:     rebuild: bool = False,
236: ) -> ProjectionSyncResult:
237:     """Advance one projection after normal registry attestation.
238: 
239:     Catch-up is the only supported operation.  Existing inconsistent state is
240:     refused; this coordinator has no path that discards rows or identity
241:     markers.  A projection already at or beyond the target is validated and
242:     reported at its actual prefix without moving backward.
243:     """
244:     if descriptor.role not in (Profile.AUTHORITY, Profile.REPLICA):
245:         raise NotAuthority(
246:             "projection maintenance requires an explicit Authority or Replica role"
247:         )
248: 
249:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
250:     maintenance: ProjectionMaintenance | None = None
251:     try:
252:         if not isinstance(ledger, AttestedLedger):
253:             raise TypeError("BackendRegistry.open must return an AttestedLedger")
254:         captured = _captured_head(ledger)
255:         target = _verify_target(
256:             ledger, captured, captured if through is None else through
257:         )
258: 
259:         maintenance = _require_catch_up(registry, descriptor, rebuild=rebuild)
260: 
261:         projected_before, before_generation = _snapshot_state(
262:             query, ledger, bound=captured
263:         )
264:         if (
265:             projected_before is not None
266:             and projected_before.lineage == target.lineage
267:             and projected_before.ordinal >= target.ordinal
268:         ):
269:             generation = (
270:                 None
271:                 if projected_before.ordinal > captured.ordinal
272:                 else before_generation
273:             )
274:             return ProjectionSyncResult(
275:                 captured_head=captured,
276:                 target=target,
277:                 projected_before=projected_before,
278:                 projected_after=projected_before,
279:                 view_generation=generation,
280:                 changed=False,
281:             )
282: 
283:         try:
284:             advance = maintenance.catch_up(target)
285:             projected_after = _resolve_head(ledger, advance.after)
286:             if projected_after is None:  # the adapter contract requires one
287:                 raise TypeError("maintenance returned no resulting watermark")
288:             if projected_after.lineage != target.lineage:
289:                 raise NotAuthority("maintenance returned a foreign projection lineage")
290:             if projected_after.ordinal < target.ordinal:
291:                 raise HeadMismatch("maintenance returned a prefix behind its target")
292:             if projected_after.ordinal == target.ordinal and projected_after != target:
293:                 raise HeadMismatch(
294:                     "maintenance returned a same-height head different from its target"
295:                 )
296:             projected_before = _resolve_head(ledger, advance.before)
297:             observed_after, generation = _snapshot_state(
298:                 query, ledger, bound=projected_after
299:             )
300:             if (
301:                 observed_after is None
302:                 or observed_after.lineage != projected_after.lineage
303:                 or observed_after.ordinal < projected_after.ordinal
304:             ):
305:                 raise HeadMismatch(
306:                     "query projection does not reach the prefix maintenance returned"
307:                 )
308:             if observed_after != projected_after:
309:                 # Another serialized maintainer advanced after this handle
310:                 # committed. The first snapshot's generation is bounded at
311:                 # our returned prefix, so it cannot identify the newer view.
312:                 # Report the attested actual prefix and leave generation
313:                 # unknown rather than mislabel it or report success as loss.
314:                 projected_after = observed_after
315:                 generation = None
316:             return ProjectionSyncResult(
317:                 captured_head=captured,
318:                 target=target,
319:                 projected_before=projected_before,
320:                 projected_after=projected_after,
321:                 view_generation=generation,
322:                 changed=advance.changed,
323:             )
324:         except Exception as cause:
325:             observed_after = None
326:             with suppress(Exception):
327:                 observed_after, _ = _snapshot_state(query, ledger, bound=captured)
328:             raise ProjectionSyncError(
329:                 f"projection maintenance through ordinal {target.ordinal} failed; "
330:                 "inspect observed_after before retrying",
331:                 target=target,
332:                 projected_before=projected_before,
333:                 observed_after=observed_after,
334:                 cause=cause,
335:                 coordinator_phase="derived-sync",
336:                 effects={
337:                     "derived": {"attempt": "entered", "state": "unknown"}
338:                 },
339:             ) from cause
340:     finally:
341:         if maintenance is not None:
342:             _close_quietly(maintenance)
343:         _close_quietly(query)
344:         _close_quietly(ledger)

=== FULL FILE libs/sdk/src/sdk/adopt.py ===
1: """Explicit adoption of a reviewed declaration into an Arrival history.
2: 
3: This is deliberately a narrow wrapper around the engine ceremony.  It does
4: not read migration sidecar inputs, mint keys, import legacy material, or infer
5: a reviewed declaration from the locator cache.  Callers bring the reviewed
6: snapshot, the published authority cache, and the selected, already-verified
7: Arrival head.
8: """
9: 
10: from __future__ import annotations
11: 
12: import hashlib
13: from collections.abc import Sequence
14: from pathlib import Path
15: from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
16: 
17: from custody.signing import ARRIVAL_DOMAIN, FACT_DOMAIN
18: from engine.arrival_contract import Head, Profile
19: from engine.arrival_registry import BackendRegistry
20: from engine.credentials import CredentialProvider, WriteCredentials
21: 
22: from .types import (
23:     ArrivalAdoptionResult,
24:     SdkValueError,
25:     StoreDescriptorInfo,
26:     TargetUnsupported,
27: )
28: 
29: if TYPE_CHECKING:
30:     from engine.arrival_adoption import ArrivalAdoptionResult as EngineArrivalAdoptionResult
31:     from engine.arrival_maintenance import ProjectionSyncResult
32:     from engine.credentials import CredentialBindingEvidence
33: 
34: __all__ = ["adopt_arrival", "recover_arrival_adoption"]
35: 
36: 
37: def _mapped_adoption_credentials(
38:     vertex_path: Path,
39:     credentials: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials | None,
40: ) -> WriteCredentials:
41:     """Resolve only an already-created mapped binding for the adoption author."""
42:     if credentials is None:
43:         raise SdkValueError("Arrival adoption requires a pre-created mapped credential binding")
44:     if isinstance(credentials, WriteCredentials):
45:         supplied = credentials
46:     else:
47:         provider = getattr(credentials, "for_write", None)
48:         if not callable(provider):
49:             raise SdkValueError("Arrival adoption requires a mapped credential provider")
50:         try:
51:             supplied = provider(vertex_path)
52:         except SdkValueError:
53:             raise
54:         except Exception as exc:
55:             raise SdkValueError(
56:                 "Arrival adoption could not resolve its mapped credential binding"
57:             ) from exc
58:     if not isinstance(supplied, WriteCredentials):
59:         raise SdkValueError("credential provider did not return WriteCredentials")
60:     if not supplied.mapped:
61:         raise SdkValueError("Arrival adoption requires a pre-created mapped credential binding")
62:     return supplied
63: 
64: 
65: def _domain_verifier(domain: str):
66:     """Verify a public signature in one explicit protocol domain."""
67:     from sign import ed25519
68: 
69:     def verify(key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
70:         try:
71:             public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
72:         except ValueError:
73:             return False
74:         return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=domain)
75: 
76:     return verify
77: 
78: 
79: def _head_dict(head: Head | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
80:     if head is None:
81:         return None
82:     return {
83:         "lineage": head.lineage,
84:         "ordinal": head.ordinal,
85:         "record_hash": head.record_hash,
86:     }
87: 
88: 
89: def _projection_dict(projection: ProjectionSyncResult | None) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
90:     if projection is None:
91:         return None
92:     return {
93:         "captured_head": _head_dict(projection.captured_head),
94:         "target": _head_dict(projection.target),
95:         "projected_before": _head_dict(projection.projected_before),
96:         "projected_after": _head_dict(projection.projected_after),
97:         "view_generation": projection.view_generation,
98:         "changed": projection.changed,
99:         "rebuilt": projection.rebuilt,
100:     }
101: 
102: 
103: def _binding_dicts(bindings: Sequence[CredentialBindingEvidence]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
104:     """Copy public mapped evidence without retaining provider-private state."""
105:     result: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
106:     for evidence in bindings:
107:         request = evidence.request
108:         result.append(
109:             {
110:                 "request": {
111:                     "namespace": request.namespace,
112:                     "observer": request.observer,
113:                     "domain": request.domain.value,
114:                     "purpose": request.purpose.value,
115:                 },
116:                 "key_ref": evidence.key_ref,
117:                 "algorithm": evidence.algorithm,
118:                 "public_key": evidence.public_key,
119:                 "provenance": evidence.provenance,
120:             }
121:         )
122:     return result
123: 
124: 
125: def _result(
126:     raw: EngineArrivalAdoptionResult,
127:     *,
128:     reviewed_sha256: str | None = None,
129:     declaration_sha256: str | None = None,
130: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
131:     """Project the engine outcome into the stable SDK result shape."""
132:     descriptor = raw.descriptor
133:     lineage = raw.lineage
134:     fact_id = getattr(raw, "fact_id", lineage)
135:     intent_path = raw.intent_path
136:     return ArrivalAdoptionResult(
137:         status=raw.status,
138:         target_path=str(raw.target_path),
139:         store=StoreDescriptorInfo.from_descriptor(descriptor),
140:         basis=raw.basis,
141:         lineage=lineage,
142:         captured_head=raw.captured_head,
143:         head=raw.head,
144:         commit=raw.commit,
145:         fact_id=fact_id,
146:         intent_path=None if intent_path is None else str(intent_path),
147:         phase=raw.phase,
148:         file_written=raw.file_written,
149:         reviewed_sha256=reviewed_sha256,
150:         declaration_sha256=declaration_sha256,
151:         projection=_projection_dict(raw.projection),
152:         credential_bindings=_binding_dicts(raw.credential_bindings),
153:         observed_head=raw.observed_head,
154:     )
155: 
156: 
157: def adopt_arrival(
158:     target: Path | str,
159:     *,
160:     selected_head: Head,
161:     reviewed_text: str,
162:     reviewed_sha256: str,
163:     declaration_text: str,
164:     observer: str,
165:     credentials: CredentialProvider | WriteCredentials,
166:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
167: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
168:     """Append one reviewed declaration anchor at an explicit migration head.
169: 
170:     ``reviewed_text`` and ``reviewed_sha256`` name the pre-migration snapshot
171:     the caller approved. ``declaration_text`` is the separately captured,
172:     currently published authority cache that will be anchored only after the
173:     engine proves their declaration meanings agree. The target must resolve to
174:     an authority descriptor and the mapped binding must already exist.
175:     """
176:     from .errors import normalize_exception
177:     from .target import _arrival_descriptor
178: 
179:     try:
180:         vertex_path = Path(target).resolve()
181:         resolved = _arrival_descriptor(vertex_path)
182:         if resolved is None:
183:             raise TargetUnsupported(
184:                 f"adopt_arrival requires an explicit Arrival .vertex target: {vertex_path}"
185:             )
186:         _path, _ast, descriptor = resolved
187:         if descriptor.role is not Profile.AUTHORITY:
188:             raise SdkValueError("Arrival adoption requires an authority descriptor")
189:         write_credentials = _mapped_adoption_credentials(vertex_path, credentials)
190:         active_registry = (
191:             registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
192:         )
193:         from engine.arrival_adoption import apply_arrival_adoption, prepare_arrival_adoption
194: 
195:         plan = prepare_arrival_adoption(
196:             active_registry,
197:             descriptor,
198:             target=vertex_path,
199:             selected_head=selected_head,
200:             reviewed_text=reviewed_text,
201:             reviewed_sha256=reviewed_sha256,
202:             declaration_text=declaration_text,
203:             observer=observer,
204:             credentials=write_credentials,
205:             fact_verify=_domain_verifier(FACT_DOMAIN),
206:             arrival_verify=_domain_verifier(ARRIVAL_DOMAIN),
207:         )
208:         raw = apply_arrival_adoption(active_registry, plan)
209:     except Exception as exc:
210:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc, context={"captured_head": selected_head})
211:         if normalized is exc:
212:             raise
213:         raise normalized from exc
214:     return _result(
215:         raw,
216:         reviewed_sha256=reviewed_sha256,
217:         declaration_sha256=hashlib.sha256(declaration_text.encode("utf-8")).hexdigest(),
218:     )
219: 
220: 
221: def recover_arrival_adoption(
222:     intent: Path | str,
223:     *,
224:     registry: BackendRegistry | None = None,
225: ) -> ArrivalAdoptionResult:
226:     """Recover a retained adoption intent without resolving or re-signing keys."""
227:     from engine.arrival_adoption import recover_arrival_adoption as recover
228: 
229:     from .errors import normalize_exception
230: 
231:     active_registry = registry if registry is not None else BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
232:     try:
233:         raw = recover(
234:             active_registry,
235:             intent,
236:             fact_verify=_domain_verifier(FACT_DOMAIN),
237:             arrival_verify=_domain_verifier(ARRIVAL_DOMAIN),
238:         )
239:     except Exception as exc:
240:         normalized = normalize_exception(exc)
241:         if normalized is exc:
242:             raise
243:         raise normalized from exc
244:     return _result(raw)

=== FULL FILE libs/sdk/src/sdk/errors.py ===
1: """Normalize engine boundary failures for SDK process callers.
2: 
3: Engine deliberately keeps backend and witness refusal families separate.  The
4: SDK is the process boundary, so it translates those families into stable SDK
5: errors while retaining the identity evidence an operator needs to reconcile a
6: refusal or an outcome that may already have committed.
7: """
8: 
9: from __future__ import annotations
10: 
11: from collections.abc import Mapping
12: from pathlib import PurePath
13: from typing import Any
14: 
15: from engine.admission import AdmissionError
16: from engine.arrival import ArrivalError
17: from engine.arrival_adoption import (
18:     AdoptionCommittedIncomplete,
19:     AdoptionOutcomeUnknown,
20:     AdoptionRecoveryRequired,
21:     AdoptionStale,
22:     AdoptionUnwitnessed,
23: )
24: from engine.arrival_contract import ContractRefusal
25: from engine.arrival_declarations import (
26:     DeclarationCommittedIncomplete,
27:     DeclarationOutcomeUnknown,
28:     DeclarationStale,
29:     DeclarationUnwitnessed,
30: )
31: from engine.arrival_head_attestation import AttestationRefusal
32: from engine.arrival_head_seam import NotWitnessed
33: from engine.arrival_initialization import (
34:     InitializationCommittedIncomplete,
35:     InitializationOutcomeUnknown,
36:     InitializationUnwitnessed,
37: )
38: from engine.arrival_maintenance import ProjectionSyncError
39: from engine.arrival_restore import RestoreForwardIncomplete, RestoreForwardUnknown
40: from engine.arrival_search import SearchIndexSyncError
41: from engine.credentials import CredentialBindingRefused
42: from engine.runtime_write import (
43:     BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed,
44:     BatchWriteCommitUnknown,
45:     BatchWritePreparationRefused,
46:     PostCommitProjectionFailed,
47:     RuntimeWriteRefused,
48:     WriteCommitUnknown,

51: from .types import AdmissionFailed, SdkError
52: 
53: __all__ = [
54:     "ArrivalRefusal",
55:     "AdmissionRefusal",
56:     "CommittedOutcome",
57:     "CommittedProjectionFailed",
58:     "CommittedIncomplete",
