65: 
66: def _json_value(value: Any) -> Any:
67:     if value is None or isinstance(value, (str, int, float, bool)):
68:         return value
69:     if isinstance(value, PurePath):
70:         return str(value)
71:     # Identity diagnostics must never stringify arbitrary record/payload objects.
72:     return None
73: 
74: 
75: def _head_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
76:     fields = ("lineage", "ordinal", "record_hash")
77:     if not all(hasattr(value, field) for field in fields):
78:         return None
79:     return {field: _json_value(getattr(value, field)) for field in fields}
80: 
81: 
82: def _coverage_dict(value: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
83:     """Serialize explicit search-index coverage without backend row bodies."""
84:     through = _head_dict(getattr(value, "through", None))
85:     fields_hash = getattr(value, "fields_hash", None)
86:     schema_version = getattr(value, "schema_version", None)
87:     if through is None or not isinstance(fields_hash, str) or not isinstance(schema_version, str):
88:         return None
89:     return {
90:         "through": through,
91:         "fields_hash": fields_hash,
92:         "schema_version": schema_version,
93:     }
94: 
95: 
96: def _identity_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any]:
97:     """Copy stable coordinates without serializing mutable record payloads."""
98:     details: dict[str, Any] = {}
99:     for name in (
100:         "fact_id",
101:         "tick_id",
102:         "ordinal",
103:         "observer",
104:         "kind",
105:         "vertex",
106:         "item_index",
107:         "source_type",
108:         "lineage",
109:         "intent_path",
110:         "phase",
111:         "fields_hash",
112:         "view_generation",
113:         "tier_index",
114:     ):
115:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
116:         if value is not None:
117:             coordinate = _json_value(value)
118:             if coordinate is not None:
119:                 details[name] = coordinate
120:     for name in ("fact_ids", "tick_ids"):
121:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
122:         if value is not None:
123:             details[name] = [_json_value(item) for item in value]
124:     items = getattr(exc, "items", None)
125:     if items is not None:
126:         details["items"] = [
127:             {
128:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
129:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
130:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
131:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
132:             }
133:             for item in items
134:         ]
135:     for name in (
136:         "head",
137:         "captured_head",
138:         "presented",
139:         "before",
140:         "after",
141:         "target",
142:         "projected_before",
143:         "observed_after",
144:         "projected_head",
145:     ):
146:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
147:         if value is None:
148:             continue
149:         head = _head_dict(value)
150:         if head is not None:
151:             details[name] = head
152:     for name in ("coverage_before", "coverage_after", "observed_after"):
153:         value = getattr(exc, name, None)
154:         coverage = _coverage_dict(value)
155:         if coverage is not None:
156:             details[name] = coverage
157:     # Preserve an explicitly unavailable coordinate without claiming a head or
158:     # coverage. Missing attributes on older wrappers still remain omitted.
159:     for name in (
160:         "captured_head", "projected_head", "projected_before", "observed_after",
161:         "coverage_before", "coverage_after",
162:     ):
163:         if hasattr(exc, name) and getattr(exc, name) is None:
164:             details[name] = None
165:     commit = getattr(exc, "commit", None)
166:     if commit is not None:
167:         before = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "before", None))
168:         after = _head_dict(getattr(commit, "after", None))
169:         commit_details: dict[str, Any] = {}
170:         if before is not None:
171:             commit_details["before"] = before
172:         if after is not None:
173:             commit_details["after"] = after
174:         if commit_details:
175:             details["commit"] = commit_details
176:     return details
177: 
178: 
179: def _credential_binding_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
180:     """Extract public mapped-binding evidence from an explicit causal chain."""
181:     seen: set[int] = set()
182:     current: BaseException | None = exc
183:     for _ in range(3):
184:         if current is None or id(current) in seen:
185:             return None
186:         seen.add(id(current))
187:         if isinstance(current, CredentialBindingRefused):
188:             result: dict[str, Any] = {"reason": current.reason}
189:             request = current.request
190:             if request is not None:
191:                 result["request"] = {
192:                     "namespace": request.namespace,
193:                     "observer": request.observer,
194:                     "domain": request.domain.value,
195:                     "purpose": request.purpose.value,
196:                 }
197:             if current.captured_head is not None:
198:                 head = _head_dict(current.captured_head)
199:                 if head is not None:
200:                     result["captured_head"] = head
201:             if current.use_position is not None:
202:                 result["use_position"] = current.use_position
203:             binding = current.evidence
204:             if binding is not None:
205:                 # A malformed provider response can be the reason for refusal.
206:                 # Do not serialize arbitrary objects retained as that evidence.
207:                 result["binding"] = {
208:                     name: value if isinstance(value, str) else None
209:                     for name in ("key_ref", "algorithm", "public_key", "provenance")
210:                     for value in (getattr(binding, name, None),)
211:                 }
212:             return result
213:         current = _causal_exception(current)
214:     return None
215: 
216: 
217: def _causal_exception(exc: BaseException) -> BaseException | None:
218:     cause = getattr(exc, "cause", None)
219:     if isinstance(cause, BaseException):
220:         return cause
221:     return exc.__cause__ if isinstance(exc.__cause__, BaseException) else None
222: 
223: 
224: def _cause_details(exc: BaseException) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
225:     """Two explicit causal nodes, never implicit context or record bodies."""
226:     seen = {id(exc)}
227:     result: dict[str, Any] | None = None
228:     parent: dict[str, Any] | None = None
229:     for _ in range(2):
230:         cause = _causal_exception(exc)
231:         if cause is None or id(cause) in seen:
232:             break
233:         seen.add(id(cause))
234:         try:
235:             message = str(cause)
236:         except Exception:
237:             message = "<message unavailable>"
238:         node: dict[str, Any] = {
239:             "type": type(cause).__name__,
240:             "message": message[:512],
241:             "details": _identity_details(cause),
242:         }
243:         if len(message) > 512:
244:             node["truncated"] = True
245:         if parent is None:
246:             result = node
247:         else:
248:             parent["cause"] = node
249:         parent = node
250:         exc = cause
251:     return result
252: 
253: 
254: def _evidence_details(exc: BaseException, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
255:     """Expose coordinator proofs without deriving effects from error classes."""
256:     evidence: dict[str, Any] = {}
257:     phase = getattr(exc, "coordinator_phase", None)
258:     if isinstance(phase, str):
259:         evidence["phase"] = phase
260:     effects = getattr(exc, "effects", None)
261:     if isinstance(effects, Mapping):
262:         safe_effects = {}
263:         for resource in ("custody", "witness", "derived", "cache", "artifact", "dispatch"):
264:             effect = effects.get(resource)
265:             if not isinstance(effect, Mapping):
266:                 continue
267:             state = effect.get("state")
268:             attempt = effect.get("attempt")
269:             if state not in (
270:                 "not-attempted", "known-none", "committed", "unknown", "completed", "incomplete"
271:             ):
272:                 continue
273:             if attempt == "not-entered" and state != "not-attempted":
274:                 continue
275:             if attempt == "entered" and state == "not-attempted":
276:                 continue
277:             safe_effect = {"state": state}
278:             if attempt in ("not-entered", "entered"):
279:                 safe_effect["attempt"] = attempt
280:             safe_effects[resource] = safe_effect
281:         if safe_effects:
282:             evidence["effects"] = safe_effects
283:     cause = _cause_details(exc)
284:     if cause is not None:
285:         evidence["cause"] = cause
286:     if not evidence:
287:         return None
288:     evidence["schema"] = "loops.sdk/evidence/v1"
289:     basis_fields = (
290:         "head", "captured_head", "presented", "target", "projected_before", "projected_head",
291:         "observed_after", "coverage_before", "coverage_after", "fields_hash", "view_generation",
292:     )
293:     identity_fields = (
294:         "fact_id", "tick_id", "fact_ids", "tick_ids", "items", "item_index", "tier_index",
295:         "ordinal", "observer", "kind", "vertex", "lineage", "commit",
296:     )
297:     basis = {name: details[name] for name in basis_fields if name in details}
298:     identities = {name: details[name] for name in identity_fields if name in details}
299:     if basis:
300:         evidence["basis"] = basis
301:     if identities:
302:         evidence["identities"] = identities
303:     return evidence
304: 
305: 
306: def _context_detail(name: str, value: Any) -> Any:
307:     """Serialize only stable identity fields supplied by an SDK operation."""
308:     if name in {"fact_ids", "tick_ids"}:
309:         return [_json_value(item) for item in value]
310:     if name == "items":
311:         return [
312:             {
313:                 "fact_id": _json_value(item.fact_id),
314:                 "tick_id": _json_value(item.tick_id),
315:                 "tick_name": _json_value(item.tick_name),
316:                 "already_present": bool(item.already_present),
317:             }
318:             for item in value
319:         ]
320:     head = _head_dict(value)
321:     return head if head is not None else _json_value(value)
322: 
323: 
324: class ArrivalRefusal(SdkError):
325:     """A typed, non-committing Arrival diagnostic refusal."""
326: 
327:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
328:         super().__init__(message)
329:         self.source_type = source_type
330:         self.details = dict(details)
331: 
332:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
333:         return {
334:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
335:             "type": type(self).__name__,
336:             "message": str(self),
337:             "source_type": self.source_type,
338:             "outcome": "refused",
339:             "details": dict(self.details),
340:         }
341:

344:     """An engine admission refusal retaining its source and diagnostics."""

347:         self,

349:         *,
350:         source_type: str,

354:         vertex: str | None = None,
355:     ) -> None:
356:         super().__init__(message, observer=observer, kind=kind, vertex=vertex)
357:         self.source_type = source_type
358:         self.details = dict(details)
359: 
360:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
361:         return {
362:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
363:             "type": type(self).__name__,
364:             "message": str(self),
365:             "source_type": self.source_type,
366:             "outcome": "admission-refused",
367:             "details": dict(self.details),
368:         }

371: class CommittedOutcome(SdkError):
372:     """A durable or possibly durable operation with explicit reconciliation evidence."""
373: 
374:     outcome = "committed"
375: 
376:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
377:         super().__init__(message)
378:         self.source_type = source_type
379:         self.details = dict(details)
380: 
381:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
382:         return {
383:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
384:             "type": type(self).__name__,
385:             "message": str(self),
386:             "source_type": self.source_type,
387:             "outcome": self.outcome,
388:             "details": dict(self.details),
389:         }

416: class ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(SdkError):
417:     """Projection maintenance may have changed derived state; reconcile it."""

420: 
421:     def __init__(self, message: str, *, source_type: str, details: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
422:         super().__init__(message)
423:         self.source_type = source_type
424:         self.details = dict(details)
425: 
426:     def as_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
427:         return {
428:             "schema": "loops.sdk/error/v1",
429:             "type": type(self).__name__,
430:             "message": str(self),
431:             "source_type": self.source_type,
432:             "outcome": self.outcome,
433:             "details": dict(self.details),
434:         }

436: 
437: def normalize_exception(
438:     exc: BaseException, *, context: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None
439: ) -> BaseException:
440:     """Return a stable SDK exception for engine boundary failures.
441: 
442:     Existing SDK errors are returned unchanged.  Classification is by the
443:     engine's public exception types, never by message text.
444:     """
445:     if not isinstance(exc, Exception) or isinstance(exc, SdkError):
446:         return exc
447:     details = _identity_details(exc)
448:     context_fields = {
449:         "captured_head",
450:         "fact_id",
451:         "tick_id",
452:         "fact_ids",
453:         "tick_ids",
454:         "items",
455:         "item_index",
456:     }
457:     for name, value in (context or {}).items():
458:         if name in details or name not in context_fields:
459:             continue
460:         coordinate = _context_detail(name, value)
461:         if coordinate is not None or value is None:
462:             details[name] = coordinate
463:     details["source_type"] = type(exc).__name__
464:     credential = _credential_binding_details(exc)
465:     if credential is not None:
466:         details["credential_binding"] = credential
467:     evidence = _evidence_details(exc, details)
468:     if evidence is not None:
469:         details["evidence"] = evidence
470:     if isinstance(exc, (BatchWriteCommitUnknown, WriteCommitUnknown)):
471:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
472:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationOutcomeUnknown):
473:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
474:     if isinstance(exc, (AdoptionOutcomeUnknown, DeclarationOutcomeUnknown, RestoreForwardUnknown)):
475:         return CommittedOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
476:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationUnwitnessed):
477:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
478:     if isinstance(exc, (AdoptionUnwitnessed, DeclarationUnwitnessed)):
479:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
480:     if isinstance(exc, InitializationCommittedIncomplete):
481:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
482:     if isinstance(
483:         exc,
484:         (AdoptionCommittedIncomplete, DeclarationCommittedIncomplete, RestoreForwardIncomplete),
485:     ):
486:         return CommittedIncomplete(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
487:     if isinstance(exc, NotWitnessed):
488:         return CommittedUnwitnessed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
489:     if isinstance(exc, (AdoptionRecoveryRequired, AdoptionStale, DeclarationStale)):
490:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
491:     if isinstance(exc, BatchPostCommitProjectionFailed):
492:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
493:     if isinstance(exc, PostCommitProjectionFailed):
494:         return CommittedProjectionFailed(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
495:     if isinstance(exc, ProjectionSyncError):
496:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
497:     if isinstance(exc, SearchIndexSyncError):
498:         return ProjectionOutcomeUnknown(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
499:     if isinstance(exc, BatchWritePreparationRefused):
500:         fact = exc.item.fact
501:         if exc.item.fact_id is not None:
502:             details.setdefault("fact_id", exc.item.fact_id)
503:         details.setdefault("observer", fact.observer)
504:         details.setdefault("kind", fact.kind)
505:         details.setdefault("vertex", exc.effective_declaration.name)
506:         if exc.admission_refused:
507:             return AdmissionRefusal(
508:                 str(exc),
509:                 source_type=type(exc).__name__,
510:                 details=details,
511:                 observer=fact.observer,
512:                 kind=fact.kind,
513:                 vertex=exc.effective_declaration.name,
514:             )
515:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
516:     if isinstance(exc, AdmissionError):
517:         return AdmissionRefusal(
518:             str(exc),
519:             source_type=type(exc).__name__,
520:             details=details,
521:             observer=getattr(exc, "observer", None),
522:             kind=getattr(exc, "kind", None),
523:             vertex=getattr(exc, "vertex", None),
524:         )
525:     if isinstance(exc, CredentialBindingRefused):
526:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
527:     if isinstance(
528:         exc,
529:         (
530:             AttestationRefusal,
531:             ContractRefusal,
532:             ArrivalError,
533:             RuntimeWriteRefused,
534:         ),
535:     ):
536:         return ArrivalRefusal(str(exc), source_type=type(exc).__name__, details=details)
537:     return exc

309:             )

312:         query.close()
313:         ledger.close()

315:     if kind == "foreign-genesis":
316:         # A retained foreign declaration is inert; it does not claim this lineage.
317:         plan = prepare_arrival_adoption(registry, descriptor, **kwargs)
318:         assert plan.captured_head == commit.after

1: """SDK boundary contract for explicit Arrival declaration adoption."""

26: def _mapped_credentials() -> WriteCredentials:
27:     return WriteCredentials(
28:         binding_namespace="tenant-a",
29:         binding_resolver=lambda _request: None,
30:         signature_verifier=lambda _domain, _key, _signature, _digest: False,
31:     )

33: 
34: def _target(tmp_path: Path) -> tuple[Path, str]:
35:     target = tmp_path / "adopt.vertex"
36:     text = (
37:         'name "adopt"\n'
38:         'store "ledger.arrival" backend="file" lineage="lineage-a" role="authority"\n'
39:         "observers {\n"
40:         '  alice { key "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA=" }\n'
41:         "}\n"
42:         "loops {\n"
43:         '  item { fold { items "collect" 100 } }\n'
44:         "}\n"
45:     )
46:     target.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
47:     return target, text
48: 
49: 
50: def _raw_result(target: Path):
51:     head = Head("lineage-a", 4, "head-after")
52:     return SimpleNamespace(
53:         status="applied",
54:         target_path=target,
55:         descriptor=StoreDescriptor(
56:             backend="file",
57:             location=str(target.parent / "ledger.arrival"),
58:             lineage="lineage-a",
59:             role=Profile.AUTHORITY,
60:         ),
61:         basis=None,
62:         lineage="lineage-a",
63:         captured_head=Head("lineage-a", 3, "head-before"),
64:         head=head,
65:         commit=None,
66:         fact_id="lineage-a",
67:         intent_path=target.with_name(target.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent"),
68:         phase="completed",
69:         file_written=True,
70:         projection=None,
71:         credential_bindings=(),
72:         observed_head=None,
73:     )

76: def test_adoption_forwards_the_reviewed_and_published_snapshots_with_mapped_binding(
77:     tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch
78: ) -> None:
79:     target, declaration_text = _target(tmp_path)
80:     reviewed_text = declaration_text.replace(
81:         'store "ledger.arrival" backend="file" lineage="lineage-a" role="authority"',
82:         'store "legacy.jsonl"',
83:     )
84:     selected = Head("lineage-a", 3, "head-before")
85:     calls: dict[str, object] = {}
86:     module = ModuleType("engine.arrival_adoption")
87: 
88:     def prepare(_registry, descriptor, **kwargs):
89:         calls["descriptor"] = descriptor
90:         calls.update(kwargs)
91:         return SimpleNamespace(target_path=target)
92: 
93:     def apply(_registry, plan):
94:         assert plan.target_path == target
95:         return _raw_result(target)
96: 
97:     module.prepare_arrival_adoption = prepare
98:     module.apply_arrival_adoption = apply
99:     monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "engine.arrival_adoption", module)
100: 
101:     result = adopt_arrival(
102:         target,
103:         selected_head=selected,
104:         reviewed_text=reviewed_text,
105:         reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode()).hexdigest(),
106:         declaration_text=declaration_text,
107:         observer="alice",
108:         credentials=_mapped_credentials(),
109:     )
110: 
111:     assert calls["selected_head"] is selected
112:     assert calls["reviewed_text"] == reviewed_text
113:     assert calls["reviewed_sha256"] == hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode()).hexdigest()
114:     assert calls["declaration_text"] == declaration_text
115:     assert calls["observer"] == "alice"
116:     assert calls["credentials"].mapped is True
117:     assert callable(calls["fact_verify"])
118:     assert callable(calls["arrival_verify"])
119:     assert calls["descriptor"].role is Profile.AUTHORITY
120:     assert result.fact_id == "lineage-a"
121:     assert result.captured_head == selected
122:     assert result.reviewed_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(reviewed_text.encode()).hexdigest()
123:     assert result.declaration_sha256 == hashlib.sha256(declaration_text.encode()).hexdigest()
124:     assert result.as_dict()["intent_path"].endswith(".arrival-adopt.intent")

165:     assert not arrival_verify(key.public_b64, fact_signature, digest)

=== FULL FILE tests/test_arrival_migration_boundary.py ===
1: """Public SDK boundary checks for the offline migration sidecar.
2: 
3: These tests intentionally live in the repository integration suite: the SDK
4: must remain installable without making the quarantined ``migrate`` package a
5: runtime or test dependency.
6: """
7:

9:

23:     CredentialPurpose,

44: 
45: 
46: @pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
47: def _isolate_runtime(tmp_path: Path, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
48:     """Keep custody and projection state out of the user's environment."""
49:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_STATE_HOME", str(tmp_path / "state"))
50:     monkeypatch.setenv("XDG_CONFIG_HOME", str(tmp_path / "config"))
51:     monkeypatch.setenv("LOOPS_HOME", str(tmp_path / "loops"))
52: 
53: 
54: class _StaticCredentials:
55:     def __init__(self, pair) -> None:
56:         self.pair = pair
57: 
58:     def for_write(self, _vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials:
59:         return WriteCredentials(
60:             fact_signer=lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
61:                 self.pair, digest.encode(), domain=FACT_DOMAIN
62:             ),
63:             arrival_signer=lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
64:                 self.pair, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
65:             ),
66:         )
67: 
68: 
69: def _signer(pair):
70:     return lambda _observer, digest: ed25519.sign(
71:         pair, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN
72:     )
73: 
74: 
75: def _verifier(key: str, signature: str, digest: str) -> bool:
76:     try:
77:         public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(key)
78:     except ValueError:
79:         return False
80:     return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=ARRIVAL_DOMAIN)
81: 
82: 
83: def _write_vertex(path: Path, public_key: str, source_name: str) -> None:
84:     path.write_text(
85:         f'''name "alice"
86: store "./data/{source_name}"
87: 
88: observers {{
89:   alice {{
90:     key "{public_key}"
91:   }}
92: }}
93: 
94: loops {{
95:   concept {{ fold {{ items "collect" 100 }} }}
96: }}
97: ''',
98:         encoding="utf-8",
99:     )
100: 
101: 
102: def _write_jsonl(path: Path) -> None:
103:     rows = [
104:         {
105:             "t": "fact",
106:             "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0",
107:             "kind": "concept",
108:             "ts": 1000.0,
109:             "observer": "alice",
110:             "origin": "legacy",
111:             "payload": '{"text":"migrated"}',
112:         },
113:         {
114:             "t": "tick",
115:             "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
116:             "name": "heartbeat",
117:             "ts": 1001.0,
118:             "since": 1000.0,
119:             "origin": "system",
120:             "payload": '{"seq":1}',
121:             "prev_hash": None,
122:             "window_start": None,
123:             "fact_cursor": None,
124:             "window_hash": None,
125:         },
126:     ]
127:     path.write_text("".join(json.dumps(row, separators=(",", ":")) + "\n" for row in rows))
128: 
129: 
130: def _write_sqlite(path: Path) -> None:
131:     with sqlite3.connect(path) as conn:
132:         conn.executescript(
133:             """
134:             CREATE TABLE facts (
135:                 id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, kind TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL,
136:                 observer TEXT NOT NULL, origin TEXT NOT NULL,
137:                 payload TEXT NOT NULL, signature TEXT
138:             );
139:             CREATE TABLE ticks (
140:                 id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, name TEXT NOT NULL, ts REAL NOT NULL,
141:                 since REAL, origin TEXT NOT NULL, payload TEXT NOT NULL,
142:                 prev_hash TEXT, window_start TEXT, fact_cursor TEXT,
143:                 window_hash TEXT, signature TEXT
144:             );
145:             INSERT INTO facts VALUES
146:               ('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0', 'concept', 1000.0,
147:                'alice', 'legacy', '{"text":"migrated"}', NULL);
148:             INSERT INTO ticks VALUES
149:               ('01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1', 'heartbeat', 1001.0, 1000.0,
150:                'system', '{"seq":1}', NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL, NULL);
151:             """
152:         )
153: 
154: 
155: @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_format", ["jsonl", "sqlite"])
156: def test_migrated_descriptor_reaches_public_sdk_boundary(
157:     tmp_path: Path, source_format: str
158: ) -> None:
159:     """Migration output is structurally readable, but lacks adopted declaration history."""
160:     data = tmp_path / "data"
161:     data.mkdir()
162:     source = data / ("legacy.jsonl" if source_format == "jsonl" else "legacy.sqlite")
163:     if source_format == "jsonl":
164:         _write_jsonl(source)
165:     else:
166:         _write_sqlite(source)
167:     source_before = source.read_bytes()
168: 
169:     pair = ed25519.load_or_generate(tmp_path / "keys" / "alice")
170:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
171:     _write_vertex(vertex, pair.public_b64, source.name)
172:     outcome = run_migration(source, vertex, store_dir=data, signer=_signer(pair))
173: 
174:     # The migration's low-level records and report remain independently verifiable.
175:     assert verify_migration_report(
176:         outcome.report_path,
177:         pair.public_b64,
178:         verify=_verifier,
179:         target_path=outcome.target_path,
180:     )
181:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
182:     assert descriptor is not None
183:     assert descriptor.backend == "file"
184:     assert descriptor.lineage == outcome.lineage
185:     assert descriptor.role is not None and descriptor.role.value == "authority"
186:     target_bytes_before_sync = outcome.target_path.read_bytes()
187:     registry = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
188:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
189:     try:
190:         head = ledger.verify(Full(through=outcome.head))
191:         records = list(ledger.scan(through=head))
192:         assert head == outcome.head
193:         assert [record["k"] for record in records] == ["genesis", "fact", "tick"]
194:         genesis, fact, tick = records
195:         assert genesis["v"] == 1
196:         assert genesis["lin"] == outcome.lineage
197:         assert genesis["ord"] == 0
198:         assert genesis["prev"] is None
199:         assert genesis["at"] == 0.0
200:         assert genesis["observer"] == "alice"
201:         assert genesis["origin"] == ""
202:         assert genesis["body"] == {
203:             "protocol": 1,
204:             "lineage": outcome.lineage,
205:             "key": pair.public_b64,
206:         }
207:         assert isinstance(genesis["sig"], str) and genesis["sig"]
208:         assert isinstance(genesis["rh"], str) and len(genesis["rh"]) == 64
209:         assert fact == {
210:             "v": 1,
211:             "lin": outcome.lineage,
212:             "ord": 1,
213:             "prev": genesis["rh"],
214:             "at": 1000.0,
215:             "k": "fact",
216:             "observer": "alice",
217:             "origin": "legacy",
218:             "body": {
219:                 "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0",
220:                 "kind": "concept",
221:                 "ts": 1000.0,
222:                 "observer": "alice",
223:                 "origin": "legacy",
224:                 "payload": '{"text":"migrated"}',
225:             },
226:             "rh": fact["rh"],
227:         }
228:         assert tick == {
229:             "v": 1,
230:             "lin": outcome.lineage,
231:             "ord": 2,
232:             "prev": fact["rh"],
233:             "at": 1001.0,
234:             "k": "tick",
235:             "observer": "alice",
236:             "origin": "system",
237:             "body": {
238:                 "id": "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FT1",
239:                 "name": "heartbeat",
240:                 "ts": 1001.0,
241:                 "since": 1000.0,
242:                 "origin": "system",
243:                 "payload": '{"seq":1}',
244:                 "prev_hash": None,
245:                 "window_start": None,
246:                 "fact_cursor": None,
247:                 "window_hash": None,
248:             },
249:             "rh": tick["rh"],
250:         }
251:         assert "sig" not in fact
252:         assert "sig" not in tick
253:     finally:
254:         query.close()
255:         ledger.close()
256: 
257:     resolved = resolve_arrival_target(vertex)
258:     assert resolved.store.lineage == outcome.lineage
259:     assert resolved.store.role == "authority"
260:     verified = verify_target(vertex)
261:     assert verified.store == resolved.store
262:     assert verified.captured_head == outcome.head
263:     assert verified.verified_through == outcome.head
264:     synced = sync_target(vertex)
265:     assert synced.store == resolved.store
266:     assert synced.captured_head == outcome.head
267:     assert synced.target == outcome.head
268:     assert synced.projected_before is None
269:     assert synced.projected_after == outcome.head
270:     assert synced.agreement is True
271:     assert outcome.target_path.read_bytes() == target_bytes_before_sync
272: 
273:     # Reads and writes must not substitute the current locator for an absent
274:     # adopted declaration. These operations may create/update a derived index,
275:     # so compare the authority log and descriptor around all refusal checks.
276:     ledger_before_refusals = outcome.target_path.read_bytes()
277:     descriptor_before_refusals = vertex.read_bytes()
278:     operations = [
279:         lambda: read_summary(vertex),
280:         lambda: read_facts(vertex),
281:         lambda: read_fact_by_id(vertex, "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0"),
282:         lambda: read_ticks(vertex),
283:         lambda: inspect_declaration(vertex),
284:         lambda: edit_declaration(
285:             vertex,
286:             vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8"),
287:             observer="alice",
288:             credentials=_StaticCredentials(pair),
289:         ),
290:         lambda: emit_fact(
291:             vertex,
292:             "concept",
293:             {"text": "must refuse"},
294:             observer="alice",
295:             credentials=_StaticCredentials(pair),
296:         ),
297:     ]
298:     for operation in operations:
299:         with pytest.raises(
300:             SdkError, match="adopted declaration|historized(?: effective)? declarations?"
301:         ):
302:             operation()
303:     assert outcome.target_path.read_bytes() == ledger_before_refusals
304:     assert vertex.read_bytes() == descriptor_before_refusals
305:     assert source.read_bytes() == source_before
306: 
307: 
308: @pytest.mark.parametrize("source_format", ["jsonl", "sqlite"])
309: def test_copied_migration_adoption_and_mapped_write(
310:     tmp_path: Path, source_format: str
311: ) -> None:
312:     """Rehearse the explicit source-copy -> migration -> adoption boundary.
313: 
314:     All originals here are synthetic. SQLite is copied through its backup API;
315:     the quiescent JSONL fixture is copied byte-for-byte. Report verification is
316:     deliberately performed at S, before adoption advances the ledger.
317:     """
318:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival, export_target, read_state
319: 
320:     original = tmp_path / "original"
321:     (original / "data").mkdir(parents=True)
322:     source = original / "data" / f"legacy.{source_format}"
323:     (_write_jsonl if source_format == "jsonl" else _write_sqlite)(source)
324:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
325:         tmp_path / "rehearsal-credentials",
326:         namespace="adoption-rehearsal",
327:         receipt_observer="alice",
328:     )
329:     binding = provider.create_binding("alice", token="rehearsal-alice")
330:     original_vertex = original / "alice.vertex"
331:     _write_vertex(original_vertex, binding.public_key, source.name)
332:     original_bytes = source.read_bytes()
333:     reviewed_bytes = original_vertex.read_bytes()
334:     reviewed_hash = hashlib.sha256(reviewed_bytes).hexdigest()
335: 
336:     rehearsal = tmp_path / "rehearsal"
337:     (rehearsal / "data").mkdir(parents=True)
338:     copied_source = rehearsal / "data" / source.name
339:     if source_format == "sqlite":
340:         with sqlite3.connect(source) as src, sqlite3.connect(copied_source) as dst:
341:             src.backup(dst)
342:     else:
343:         shutil.copyfile(source, copied_source)
344:     vertex = rehearsal / "alice.vertex"
345:     vertex.write_bytes(reviewed_bytes)
346:     copied_bytes = copied_source.read_bytes()
347:     registry_binding = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
348:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL,
349:         CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
350:     ))
351:     assert registry_binding is not None
352: 
353:     def migration_signer(observer: str, digest: str) -> str:
354:         assert observer == "alice"
355:         return registry_binding.sign_digest(digest)
356: 
357:     migrated = run_migration(
358:         copied_source, vertex, store_dir=rehearsal / "data", signer=migration_signer,
359:     )
360:     assert verify_migration_report(
361:         migrated.report_path, binding.public_key, verify=_verifier,
362:         target_path=migrated.target_path,
363:     )
364:     report_bytes = migrated.report_path.read_bytes()
365:     # External provenance stays outside the declaration's protocol payload.
366:     evidence = {
367:         "reviewed_sha256": reviewed_hash,
368:         "report_sha256": hashlib.sha256(report_bytes).hexdigest(),
369:         "report_verified_at": migrated.head.record_hash,
370:     }
371:     prefix = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
372:     descriptor = resolve_arrival_target(vertex).store
373:     published_text = vertex.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
374:     sync_target(vertex)
375:     with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="adopted declaration|historized"):
376:         read_summary(vertex)
377: 
378:     # A caller cannot silently pass revised bytes under the reviewed hash.
379:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
380:         adopt_arrival(
381:             vertex, selected_head=migrated.head,
382:             reviewed_text=reviewed_bytes.decode() + "\n// unreviewed\n",
383:             reviewed_sha256=reviewed_hash, declaration_text=published_text,
384:             observer="alice", credentials=provider,
385:         )
386:     assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == prefix
387: 
388:     adopted = adopt_arrival(
389:         vertex, selected_head=migrated.head, reviewed_text=reviewed_bytes.decode(),
390:         reviewed_sha256=reviewed_hash, declaration_text=published_text,
391:         observer="alice", credentials=provider,
392:     )
393:     assert adopted.commit is not None
394:     assert adopted.commit.before == migrated.head
395:     assert adopted.head == adopted.commit.after
396:     assert adopted.head.ordinal == migrated.head.ordinal + 1
397:     assert adopted.fact_id == migrated.lineage
398:     assert resolve_arrival_target(vertex).store == descriptor
399:     after_adoption = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
400:     assert after_adoption.startswith(prefix)
401:     assert len(after_adoption.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + 1
402:     assert migrated.report_path.read_bytes() == report_bytes
403:     assert hashlib.sha256(report_bytes).hexdigest() == evidence["report_sha256"]
404: 
405:     for result in (
406:         inspect_declaration(vertex), read_summary(vertex), read_facts(vertex),
407:         read_ticks(vertex), read_state(vertex),
408:     ):
409:         assert result.basis is not None
410:         assert result.basis.captured_head == adopted.head
411:         json.dumps(result.as_dict(), allow_nan=False)
412:     facts = read_facts(vertex, include_internal=True, order="oldest", limit=100)
413:     anchors = [row for row in facts.items if row["kind"] == "_decl.genesis"]
414:     assert len(anchors) == 1 and anchors[0]["id"] == migrated.lineage
415:     original_fact = read_fact_by_id(vertex, "01ARZ3NDEKTSV4RRFFQ69G5FA0")
416:     assert original_fact.fact is not None
417: 
418:     receipt = emit_fact(
419:         vertex, "concept", {"text": "after adoption"}, observer="alice",
420:         id_override="adoption-rehearsal-followup", credentials=provider,
421:     )
422:     assert receipt.stored and receipt.signed and receipt.witnessed
423:     assert receipt.commit is not None and receipt.commit.before == adopted.head
424:     final = verify_target(vertex)
425:     assert final.verified_through == receipt.commit.after
426:     exported = rehearsal / "captured.jsonl"
427:     export = export_target(vertex, exported)
428:     assert export.head == receipt.commit.after
429:     assert exported.read_bytes() == migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
430:     for result in (adopted, receipt, final, export):
431:         json.dumps(result.as_dict(), allow_nan=False)
432:     assert source.read_bytes() == original_bytes
433:     assert original_vertex.read_bytes() == reviewed_bytes
434:     assert copied_source.read_bytes() == copied_bytes
435: 
436: 
437: @pytest.mark.parametrize("stop_phase", ["after-intent", "after-append"])
438: def test_adoption_process_exit_recovers_reserved_draft_without_credentials(
439:     tmp_path: Path, stop_phase: str, monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch,
440: ) -> None:
441:     """Exit a real child process at a durable boundary, then reconcile once."""
442:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, recover_arrival_adoption
443: 
444:     data = tmp_path / "data"
445:     data.mkdir()
446:     source = data / "legacy.jsonl"
447:     _write_jsonl(source)
448:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
449:         tmp_path / "credentials", namespace="interrupted-adoption", receipt_observer="alice",
450:     )
451:     created = provider.create_binding("alice", token="create-alice")
452:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
453:     _write_vertex(vertex, created.public_key, source.name)
454:     reviewed = vertex.read_text()
455:     signed = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
456:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
457:     ))
458:     assert signed is not None
459:     migrated = run_migration(
460:         source, vertex, store_dir=data, signer=lambda _observer, digest: signed.sign_digest(digest),
461:     )
462:     prefix = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
463:     script = r'''
464: import hashlib, json, os, sys
465: from pathlib import Path
466: import engine.arrival_adoption as engine_adoption
467: from engine.arrival_contract import Head
468: from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival
469: target, root, namespace, reviewed, head_json, stop = sys.argv[1:]
470: original_apply = engine_adoption.apply_arrival_adoption
471: def stop_at(phase):
472:     if phase == stop:
473:         os._exit(86)
474: def interrupted(registry, plan):
475:     return original_apply(registry, plan, failure_hook=stop_at)
476: engine_adoption.apply_arrival_adoption = interrupted
477: provider = MappedCredentialProvider(Path(root), namespace=namespace, receipt_observer="alice")
478: adopt_arrival(
479:     target, selected_head=Head(**json.loads(head_json)), reviewed_text=reviewed,
480:     reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
481:     declaration_text=Path(target).read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=provider,
482: )
483: raise AssertionError("durable stop phase was not reached")
484: '''
485:     run = subprocess.run(
486:         [sys.executable, "-c", script, str(vertex), str(provider.root), provider.namespace,
487:          reviewed, json.dumps({"lineage": migrated.head.lineage,
488:                                "ordinal": migrated.head.ordinal,
489:                                "record_hash": migrated.head.record_hash}), stop_phase],
490:         capture_output=True, text=True, timeout=30, check=False,
491:     )
492:     assert run.returncode == 86, run.stderr
493:     intent = vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent")
494:     assert intent.exists()
495:     reserved = json.loads(intent.read_text())["drafts"][0]
496:     before_recovery = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
497:     expected_extra = 1 if stop_phase == "after-append" else 0
498:     assert len(before_recovery.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + expected_extra
499: 
500:     # Recovery cannot depend on the original private-key location or provider.
501:     provider.root.rename(tmp_path / "credentials-offline")
502:     def refuse_credentials(*_args, **_kwargs):
503:         raise AssertionError("recovery resolved signing credentials")
504:     monkeypatch.setattr(MappedCredentialProvider, "for_write", refuse_credentials)
505:     monkeypatch.setattr(MappedCredentialProvider, "resolve", refuse_credentials)
506:     authentic_intent = intent.read_bytes()
507:     for mutation in ("binding", "inner-signature", "outer-signature"):
508:         changed = json.loads(authentic_intent)
509:         if mutation == "binding":
510:             changed["bindings"][0]["public_key"] = "AAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAAA="
511:         elif mutation == "inner-signature":
512:             changed["drafts"][0]["body"]["signature"] = "bogus"
513:         else:
514:             changed["drafts"][0]["signature"] = "bogus"
515:         # Keep the duplicate draft representations consistent: consistency
516:         # alone must not stand in for signature and captured-key verification.
517:         changed["exact_drafts"] = json.dumps(
518:             changed["drafts"], ensure_ascii=False, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"),
519:         )
520:         intent.write_text(json.dumps(changed))
521:         with pytest.raises(SdkError):
522:             recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
523:         assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == before_recovery
524:         assert intent.exists()
525:     intent.write_bytes(authentic_intent)
526:     recovered = recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
527:     assert recovered.head is not None
528:     assert recovered.head.ordinal == migrated.head.ordinal + 1
529:     assert recovered.captured_head == migrated.head
530:     assert recovered.commit is not None
531:     assert recovered.commit.before == migrated.head
532:     assert recovered.commit.after == recovered.head
533:     final = migrated.target_path.read_bytes()
534:     assert final.startswith(prefix)
535:     assert len(final.splitlines()) == len(prefix.splitlines()) + 1
536:     record = json.loads(final.splitlines()[-1])
537:     assert record["body"] == reserved["body"]
538:     assert record["sig"] == reserved["signature"]
539:     assert not intent.exists()
540:     assert read_summary(vertex).basis.captured_head == recovered.head
541:     with pytest.raises(SdkError):
542:         recover_arrival_adoption(intent)
543:     assert migrated.target_path.read_bytes() == final
544:

546: def _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path: Path):
547:     """A real signed migration for adoption's SDK boundary fault tests."""
548:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider
549: 
550:     data = tmp_path / "data"
551:     data.mkdir()
552:     source = data / "legacy.jsonl"
553:     _write_jsonl(source)
554:     provider = MappedCredentialProvider(
555:         tmp_path / "credentials", namespace="adoption-faults", receipt_observer="alice",
556:     )
557:     binding = provider.create_binding("alice", token="create-alice")
558:     vertex = tmp_path / "alice.vertex"
559:     _write_vertex(vertex, binding.public_key, source.name)
560:     reviewed = vertex.read_text()
561:     signer = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
562:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, CredentialPurpose.INITIALIZATION,
563:     ))
564:     assert signer is not None
565:     migration = run_migration(
566:         source, vertex, store_dir=data, signer=lambda _observer, digest: signer.sign_digest(digest),
567:     )
568:     return provider, binding, vertex, reviewed, migration

571: @pytest.mark.parametrize("broken_domain", [SigningDomain.FACT, SigningDomain.ARRIVAL])
572: def test_adoption_independently_verifies_custom_mapped_signatures(
573:     tmp_path: Path, broken_domain: SigningDomain,
574: ) -> None:
575:     from engine.credentials import ResolvedCredential
576:     from sdk import adopt_arrival
577: 
578:     provider, _binding, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
579:     before = migration.target_path.read_bytes()
580: 
581:     def resolve(request):
582:         genuine = provider.resolve(request)
583:         assert genuine is not None
584:         if request.domain is broken_domain:
585:             return ResolvedCredential(genuine.evidence, lambda _digest: "junk-signature")
586:         return genuine
587: 
588:     untrustworthy = WriteCredentials(
589:         binding_namespace=provider.namespace,
590:         binding_resolver=resolve,
591:         signature_verifier=lambda *_args: True,
592:     )
593:     with pytest.raises(SdkError, match="sign|verif"):
594:         adopt_arrival(
595:             vertex, selected_head=migration.head, reviewed_text=reviewed,
596:             reviewed_sha256=hashlib.sha256(reviewed.encode()).hexdigest(),
597:             declaration_text=vertex.read_text(), observer="alice", credentials=untrustworthy,
598:         )
599:     assert migration.target_path.read_bytes() == before
600:     assert not vertex.with_name(vertex.name + ".arrival-adopt.intent").exists()

603: def test_adoption_verifies_actual_binding_when_observer_has_two_valid_keys(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
604:     from engine.admission import fact_commitment_hash
605:     from engine.arrival import content_commitment
606:     from engine.arrival_contract import RecordDraft
607:     from sdk import MappedCredentialProvider, adopt_arrival
608: 
609:     provider, original, vertex, reviewed, migration = _mapped_migration_fixture(tmp_path)
610:     selected = MappedCredentialProvider(
611:         tmp_path / "second-credentials", namespace="selected-key", receipt_observer="alice",
612:     )
613:     second = selected.create_binding("alice", token="create-second-key")
614:     signer = provider.resolve(CredentialRequest(
615:         provider.namespace, "alice", SigningDomain.ARRIVAL, CredentialPurpose.KEY_INTRODUCTION,
616:     ))
617:     assert signer is not None
618:     body = {"observer": "alice", "key": second.public_key}
619:     digest = content_commitment("key", 1002.0, "alice", "", body)
620:     registry = BackendRegistry.with_builtin_backends()
621:     descriptor = descriptor_for(parse_vertex_file(vertex), vertex)
622:     assert descriptor is not None
623:     ledger, query = registry.open(descriptor)
624:     try:
625:         introduction = ledger.append(migration.head, (
626:             RecordDraft("key", 1002.0, "alice", "", body, signer.sign_digest(digest)),
627:         ))
628:     finally:
629:         query.close()
630:         ledger.close()
631:     result = adopt_arrival(
632:         vertex, selected_head=introduction.after, reviewed_text=reviewed,

=== SELECTED DEFINITIONS libs/lang/src/lang/document.py ===
699: def vertex_to_documents(ast: VertexFile) -> list[Document]:
700:     """Decompose a ``VertexFile`` AST into per-subject declaration documents.
701: 
702:     Emission order (deterministic): the ``_decl.vertex-defined`` singleton,
703:     then ``_decl.kind-defined`` per loop, ``_decl.observer-defined`` per
704:     observer, ``_decl.member-defined`` per combine entry, ``_decl.source-defined``
705:     per source (bare/template entries first, then inline sources per block),
706:     and ``_decl.lens-defined`` if a lens is declared.
707: 
708:     Each multi-instance document carries an ``order`` field (encounter
709:     position) and a subject deduplicated per kind (see module docstring:
710:     "Order is meaning", "Subject uniqueness"). Residence (``store`` locator,
711:     ``path``) is excluded. Every payload is validated JSON-safe before return.
712:     """
713:     docs: list[Document] = []
714:     order = 0  # global encounter position across all multi-instance documents
715: 
716:     # Per-kind subject deduplication. Allocate against the SET of subjects
717:     # already issued for this kind (not a per-base counter) so a suffix never
718:     # lands on a naturally-occurring subject: bases ["base", "base", "base#2"]
719:     # → ["base", "base#2", "base#3"], all distinct. Identity only — projection
720:     # reconstructs every value from the payload, never from the subject.
721:     issued: dict[str, set[str]] = {}
722: 
723:     def _subject(kind: str, base: str) -> str:
724:         seen = issued.setdefault(kind, set())
725:         candidate = base
726:         n = 1
727:         while candidate in seen:
728:             n += 1
729:             candidate = f"{base}#{n}"
730:         seen.add(candidate)
731:         return candidate
732: 
733:     def _emit(kind: str, base: str, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
734:         nonlocal order
735:         payload["order"] = order
736:         order += 1
737:         docs.append(Document(kind=kind, subject=_subject(kind, base), payload=payload))
738: 
739:     # vertex-defined singleton — subject is the vertex name (self); no order.
740:     docs.append(
741:         Document(
742:             kind=DECL_VERTEX_DEFINED,
743:             subject=ast.name,
744:             payload={
745:                 "name": ast.name,
746:                 "strict": ast.strict,
747:                 "observer_scoped": ast.observer_scoped,
748:                 "discover": ast.discover,
749:                 "emit": ast.emit,
750:                 "routes": dict(ast.routes) if ast.routes is not None else None,
751:                 "vertices": [str(p) for p in ast.vertices]
752:                 if ast.vertices is not None
753:                 else None,
754:                 "boundary": [_boundary_to_json(b) for b in ast.boundary],
755:                 # sources live in their own documents; this flag preserves the
756:                 # None-vs-empty-tuple distinction of VertexFile.sources.
757:                 "sources_present": ast.sources is not None,
758:                 # authoritative block structure (index + mode); None when there
759:                 # are no sources_blocks. Members are the inline source docs.
760:                 "source_blocks": [
761:                     {"index": i, "mode": b.mode}
762:                     for i, b in enumerate(ast.sources_blocks)
763:                 ]
764:                 if ast.sources_blocks is not None
765:                 else None,
766:             },
767:         )
768:     )
769: 
770:     # kind-defined per loop (dict preserves insertion order).
771:     for kind_name, loop in ast.loops.items():
772:         _emit(DECL_KIND_DEFINED, kind_name, _loop_def_to_payload(loop))
773: 
774:     # observer-defined per observer.
775:     for obs in ast.observers or ():
776:         _emit(DECL_OBSERVER_DEFINED, obs.name, _observer_to_payload(obs))
777: 
778:     # member-defined per combine entry.
779:     for entry in ast.combine or ():
780:         _emit(DECL_MEMBER_DEFINED, entry.name, _combine_to_payload(entry))
781: 
782:     # source-defined — bare/template entries from VertexFile.sources ...
783:     base = ast.path.parent if ast.path is not None else None
784:     for entry in ast.sources or ():
785:         if isinstance(entry, TemplateSource):
786:             payload = _template_source_to_payload(entry, base=base)
787:         else:
788:             payload = {
789:                 "form": "path",
790:                 "path": str(entry),
791:                 "content_sha256": _content_sha256(Path(entry), base),
792:             }
793:         _emit(DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED, _source_subject(entry), payload)
794: 
795:     # ... then inline sources from VertexFile.sources_blocks, tagged with
796:     # their block index so projection can rebuild block grouping.
797:     for block_idx, block in enumerate(ast.sources_blocks or ()):
798:         # ast.py's custom frozen decorator rebuilds classes dynamically, so
799:         # Pyright can't resolve SourcesBlock.sources' type (infers Never).
800:         for src in block.sources:  # pyright: ignore[reportGeneralTypeIssues]
801:             _emit(
802:                 DECL_SOURCE_DEFINED,
803:                 _source_subject(src),
804:                 _inline_source_to_payload(src, block=block_idx, mode=block.mode),
805:             )
806: 
807:     # lens-defined singleton (whole-vertex fold/stream selection).
808:     if ast.lens is not None:
809:         _emit(DECL_LENS_DEFINED, ast.name, _lens_to_payload(ast.lens))
810: 
811:     # Enforce JSON safety (finite floats, str keys, JSON scalars) at the source.
812:     for d in docs:
813:         _ensure_json_safe(d.payload, f"{d.kind}:{d.subject}")
814: 
815:     return docs

=== SELECTED DEFINITIONS libs/lang/src/lang/validator.py ===
300: def validate_vertex_file(vertex: VertexFile) -> list[ValidationError]:
301:     """Validate a .vertex file.
302: 
303:     Returns list of errors.
304:     """
305:     ctx = ValidationContext(path=str(vertex.path) if vertex.path else None)
306: 
307:     # Reserved declaration namespace (SPEC §9.2 / build-plan write-time
308:     # reservation): the ``_decl.*`` prefix is the internal-table vocabulary,
309:     # recorded only through the genesis/absorb ceremony — never user-declared.
310:     # Reject at the grammar layer so a ``.vertex`` cannot mint a kind (a loop
311:     # definition or a source's emit kind) that collides with the protocol's
312:     # own rows. The single ``is_internal_kind`` predicate is the one place the
313:     # prefix is spelled (build-plan "SQL wildcard trap").
314:     from .document import DECL_PREFIX, is_internal_kind
315: 
316:     for loop_name in vertex.loops:
317:         if is_internal_kind(loop_name):
318:             ctx.error(
319:                 f"loop '{loop_name}' uses the reserved declaration namespace "
320:                 f"('{DECL_PREFIX}*') — recorded via `sl store absorb`, "
321:                 f"never declared",
322:             )
323:     for block in vertex.sources_blocks or []:
324:         for src in block.sources:
325:             if is_internal_kind(src.kind):
326:                 ctx.error(
327:                     f"source emits kind '{src.kind}' in the reserved "
328:                     f"declaration namespace ('{DECL_PREFIX}*') — not permitted",
329:                 )
330:     for route_kind in vertex.routes or {}:
331:         if is_internal_kind(route_kind):
332:             ctx.error(
333:                 f"route key '{route_kind}' is in the reserved declaration "
334:                 f"namespace ('{DECL_PREFIX}*') — not permitted",
335:             )
336: 
337:     # Check routes reference defined loops (skip for combine vertices —
338:     # routes only make sense with local loops, not cross-store reads)
339:     if vertex.routes and vertex.combine is None:
340:         for kind, loop_name in vertex.routes.items():
341:             if loop_name not in vertex.loops:
342:                 ctx.error(
343:                     f"route '{kind}' references undefined loop '{loop_name}'",
344:                     hint=f"defined loops: {', '.join(vertex.loops.keys())}",
345:                 )
346: 
347:     # Check each loop definition
348:     for loop_name, loop_def in vertex.loops.items():
349:         # Validate fold declarations
350:         if not loop_def.folds and loop_def.boundary is None and not loop_def.search:
351:             ctx.error(f"loop '{loop_name}' has no fold declarations")
352: 
353:         # Check for duplicate fold targets
354:         targets = [f.target for f in loop_def.folds]
355:         seen = set()
356:         for target in targets:
357:             if target in seen:
358:                 ctx.error(f"loop '{loop_name}' has duplicate fold target '{target}'")
359:             seen.add(target)
360: 
361:         # Validate per-kind parse pipeline — vertex receives dicts, not raw lines
362:         if loop_def.parse:
363:             validate_parse_flow(
364:                 loop_def.parse, ctx, initial_shape=Shape(ShapeKind.DICT),
365:             )
366: 
367:     # Check sources blocks
368:     for block in vertex.sources_blocks or []:
369:         if not block.sources:
370:             ctx.error(f"sources {block.mode} block has no sources")
371:         # Check for duplicate kinds within a block
372:         kinds = [s.kind for s in block.sources]
373:         seen_kinds: set[str] = set()
374:         for kind in kinds:
375:             if kind in seen_kinds:
376:                 ctx.error(f"sources {block.mode} block has duplicate kind '{kind}'")
377:             seen_kinds.add(kind)
378: 
379:     return ctx.errors

398: def validate_vertex(vertex: VertexFile) -> None:
399:     """Validate a .vertex file, raising on first error."""
400:     errors = validate_vertex_file(vertex)
401:     if errors:
402:         raise errors[0]

=== SELECTED DEFINITIONS libs/engine/src/engine/arrival_body.py ===
135: def _validate_row(obj: object, kind: str) -> None:
136:     fault = _row_fault(obj, kind)
137:     if fault is not None:
138:         _bad(fault)

141: def _row_of(obj: dict, kind: str) -> tuple:
142:     """A VALIDATED body as its full-arity row tuple, signature last."""
143:     return (*(obj[f] for f in _FIELDS[kind]), obj.get(SIGNATURE_FIELD))

146: def _encode(row: tuple, kind: str) -> dict:
147:     """Build the body for a row — and hold it to the decoder's rules.
148: 
149:     ``encode(x)`` must always be decodable, the line codec's symmetry and
150:     for its reason: a row with a *typed*-wrong field (a fact carrying
151:     ``ts="1.0"``, which sqlite's REAL affinity accepts) would otherwise get
152:     a durable arrival record its own reader then refuses. Validating the
153:     built object puts the error at the append site, where it is
154:     attributable to the caller that assembled the row.
155:     """
156:     fields = _FIELDS[kind]
157:     n = len(fields)
158:     if len(row) not in (n, n + 1):
159:         _bad(f"{kind} row must have {n} or {n + 1} fields, got {len(row)}")
160:     body: dict = dict(zip(fields, row[:n], strict=True))
161:     if len(row) > n and row[n] is not None:
162:         body[SIGNATURE_FIELD] = row[n]
163:     _validate_row(body, kind)
164:     return body

167: def body_of_fact_row(row: tuple) -> dict:
168:     """A fact row ``(id, kind, ts, observer, origin, payload[, signature])``
169:     as its VALIDATED arrival body."""
170:     return _encode(row, FACT_KIND)

269: def rows_of_body(k: str, body: object) -> list[tuple[str, tuple]]:
270:     """The store rows one arrival body expands to, in on-the-wire order.
271: 
272:     Dispatches on the ENVELOPE's ``k``, which is the whole point of dropping
273:     ``t``: the record class is stated once, by the record, and the body is
274:     read as whatever the envelope already said it is. A ``fact`` or ``tick``
275:     yields one ``(t, row)``; a ``batch`` yields its rows expanded in array
276:     order (always two or more).
277: 
278:     The returned ``t`` is the ROW class, so a batch's rows come back as
279:     ``"fact"`` — callers index rows, and a batch is an atomicity envelope,
280:     not a row class.
281:     """
282:     if k == BATCH_KIND:
283:         _validate_batch(body)
284:         assert isinstance(body, dict)
285:         return [(FACT_KIND, _row_of(elem, FACT_KIND)) for elem in body[_ROWS]]
286:     if k not in _FIELDS:
287:         _bad(
288:             f"record kind {k!r} carries no rows — a body is read as the kind "
289:             "its envelope names, and this one names no row class"
290:         )
291:     _validate_row(body, k)
292:     assert isinstance(body, dict)
293:     return [(k, _row_of(body, k))]

=== SELECTED DEFINITIONS libs/custody/src/custody/binding.py ===
229: class MappedCredentialProvider:
230:     """Resolve explicit namespace/observer bindings without locator inference.
231: 
232:     ``resolve`` and ``verify`` are read-only.  Binding creation, import, and
233:     existing-reference publication are separate durable mutations serialized by
234:     one provider-root advisory lock.
235:     """
236: 
237:     def __init__(
238:         self,
239:         root: Path | str,
240:         *,
241:         namespace: str,
242:         receipt_observer: str | None = None,
243:         _creation_hook: Callable[[str, dict[str, object]], None] | None = None,
244:     ) -> None:
245:         self.root = Path(root)
246:         self.namespace = _exact(namespace, "namespace")
247:         self.receipt_observer = (
248:             _exact(receipt_observer, "receipt_observer")
249:             if receipt_observer is not None
250:             else None
251:         )
252:         # Test-only fault injection. It is intentionally absent from public
253:         # results and never called by read resolution.
254:         self._creation_hook = _creation_hook
255: 
256:     @property
257:     def mapped(self) -> bool:
258:         """Allow SDK compatibility arms to refuse mapped-to-legacy downgrade."""
259:         return True
260: 
261:     def for_write(self, vertex: Path) -> WriteCredentials:
262:         """Return lazy mapped credentials; ``vertex`` selects no local key."""
263:         del vertex
264:         return WriteCredentials(
265:             binding_namespace=self.namespace,
266:             receipt_observer=self.receipt_observer,
267:             binding_resolver=self.resolve,
268:             signature_verifier=self.verify,
269:         )
270: 
271:     def _slot(self, namespace: str, observer: str) -> str:
272:         return _digest(namespace, observer)
273: 
274:     def _binding_path(self, namespace: str, observer: str) -> Path:
275:         return self.root / "bindings-v1" / f"{self._slot(namespace, observer)}.json"
276: 
277:     def _pending_path(self, namespace: str, observer: str) -> Path:
278:         return self.root / "pending-v1" / f"{self._slot(namespace, observer)}.json"
279: 
280:     def _intent_path(self, token: str) -> Path:
281:         return self.root / "intents-v1" / f"{_digest(token)}.json"
282: 
283:     def _key_dir(self, key_ref: str) -> Path:
284:         if not _KEY_REF.fullmatch(key_ref):
285:             raise ValueError("mapped custody key reference is malformed")
286:         return self.root / "keys-v1" / key_ref
287: 
288:     def _root_dir(self, *, create: bool) -> bool:
289:         """Validate the configured root without making reads persistent."""
290:         mode = _lstat_kind(self.root)
291:         if mode is None:
292:             if not create:
293:                 return False
294:             _directory(self.root, create=True)
295:         elif not S_ISDIR(mode):
296:             raise ValueError("mapped custody root is not a directory")
297:         return True
298: 
299:     def _subdir(self, name: str, *, create: bool) -> Path | None:
300:         if not self._root_dir(create=create):
301:             return None
302:         path = self.root / name
303:         mode = _lstat_kind(path)
304:         if mode is None:
305:             if not create:
306:                 return None
307:             _directory(path, create=True)
308:         elif not S_ISDIR(mode):
309:             raise ValueError(f"mapped custody path is not a directory: {path}")
310:         return path
311: 
312:     @contextmanager
313:     def _mutation_lock(self) -> Iterator[None]:
314:         self._root_dir(create=True)
315:         lock_path = self.root / ".bindings-v1.lock"
316:         flags = os.O_RDWR | os.O_CREAT | getattr(os, "O_NOFOLLOW", 0)
317:         fd = os.open(lock_path, flags, 0o600)
318:         try:
319:             if not S_ISREG(os.fstat(fd).st_mode):
320:                 raise ValueError("mapped custody lock is not a regular file")
321:             fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX)
322:             yield
323:         finally:
324:             fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)
325:             os.close(fd)
326: 
327:     def _read_json(self, path: Path) -> dict[str, object] | None:
328:         if not _regular(path, required=False):
329:             return None
330:         try:
331:             def reject_duplicates(pairs: list[tuple[str, object]]) -> dict[str, object]:
332:                 result: dict[str, object] = {}
333:                 for key, value in pairs:
334:                     if key in result:
335:                         raise ValueError(f"duplicate field {key!r}")
336:                     result[key] = value
337:                 return result
338: 
339:             loaded = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"), object_pairs_hook=reject_duplicates)
340:         except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
341:             raise ValueError(f"malformed mapped custody record: {path}") from exc
342:         if not isinstance(loaded, dict):
343:             raise TypeError(f"mapped custody record is not an object: {path}")
344:         return loaded
345: 
346:     def _binding_record(self, namespace: str, observer: str) -> dict[str, object] | None:
347:         parent = self._subdir("bindings-v1", create=False)
348:         if parent is None:
349:             return None
350:         record = self._read_json(parent / f"{self._slot(namespace, observer)}.json")
351:         if record is None:
352:             return None
353:         expected = {
354:             "schema": _SCHEMA,
355:             "namespace": namespace,
356:             "observer": observer,
357:         }
358:         if any(record.get(key) != value for key, value in expected.items()):
359:             raise ValueError("mapped custody binding does not match its slot")
360:         for key in ("key_ref", "algorithm", "public_key", "provenance"):
361:             if not isinstance(record.get(key), str) or not record[key]:
362:                 raise ValueError(f"mapped custody binding lacks {key}")
363:         if record["algorithm"] != "ed25519":
364:             raise ValueError("mapped custody binding has unsupported algorithm")
365:         self._key_dir(str(record["key_ref"]))
366:         return record
367: 
368:     def _load_keypair(self, key_ref: str, *, require_public_file: bool = False):
369:         keys = self._subdir("keys-v1", create=False)
370:         if keys is None:
371:             raise FileNotFoundError(self.root / "keys-v1")
372:         key_dir = keys / key_ref
373:         mode = _lstat_kind(key_dir)
374:         if mode is None or not S_ISDIR(mode):
375:             raise FileNotFoundError(key_dir)
376:         private_path = key_dir / "ed25519.key"
377:         _regular(private_path)
378:         pair = ed25519.load(key_dir)
379:         public_path = key_dir / "ed25519.pub"
380:         exists = _regular(public_path, required=False)
381:         if require_public_file and not exists:
382:             raise ValueError("mapped custody key is missing its public evidence")
383:         if exists and public_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8") != pair.public_b64 + "\n":
384:             raise ValueError("mapped custody public evidence disagrees with private key")
385:         return pair
386: 
387:     def _validated_binding(self, namespace: str, observer: str) -> dict[str, object] | None:
388:         record = self._binding_record(namespace, observer)
389:         if record is None:
390:             return None
391:         pair = self._load_keypair(str(record["key_ref"]))
392:         if pair.public_b64 != record["public_key"]:
393:             raise ValueError("mapped custody binding public key disagrees with private key")
394:         return record
395: 
396:     def _completed_key_reference(self, key_ref: str, expected_public_key: str) -> None:
397:         """Require explicit reuse to name a published, validated binding."""
398:         bindings = self._subdir("bindings-v1", create=False)
399:         if bindings is None:
400:             raise FileNotFoundError("no completed mapped binding names this key reference")
401:         found = False
402:         for path in bindings.iterdir():
403:             if path.suffix != ".json":
404:                 continue
405:             record = self._read_json(path)
406:             if record is None or record.get("key_ref") != key_ref:
407:                 continue
408:             if (
409:                 record.get("schema") != _SCHEMA
410:                 or not isinstance(record.get("namespace"), str)
411:                 or not isinstance(record.get("observer"), str)
412:                 or record.get("public_key") != expected_public_key
413:             ):
414:                 raise ValueError("published mapped binding is malformed")
415:             slot = f"{self._slot(str(record['namespace']), str(record['observer']))}.json"
416:             if path.name != slot:
417:                 raise ValueError("published mapped binding is stored in the wrong slot")
418:             pair = self._load_keypair(key_ref, require_public_file=True)
419:             if pair.public_b64 != expected_public_key:
420:                 raise ValueError("published mapped binding disagrees with its key object")
421:             found = True
422:         if not found:
423:             raise FileNotFoundError("no completed mapped binding names this key reference")
424: 
425:     def _evidence(
426:         self, request: CredentialRequest, record: dict[str, object]
427:     ) -> CredentialBindingEvidence:
428:         return CredentialBindingEvidence(
429:             request=request,
430:             key_ref=str(record["key_ref"]),
431:             algorithm="ed25519",
432:             public_key=str(record["public_key"]),
433:             provenance=str(record["provenance"]),
434:         )
435: 
436:     def resolve(self, request: CredentialRequest) -> ResolvedCredential | None:
437:         """Resolve one credential without locks, mkdirs, repair, or fallback."""
438:         if request.namespace != self.namespace:
439:             raise CredentialBindingRefused("request-mismatch", request=request)
440:         try:
441:             record = self._validated_binding(request.namespace, request.observer)
442:             if record is None:
443:                 pending_dir = self._subdir("pending-v1", create=False)
444:                 pending = (
445:                     None
446:                     if pending_dir is None
447:                     else self._read_json(pending_dir / f"{self._slot(request.namespace, request.observer)}.json")
448:                 )
449:                 if pending is not None:
450:                     raise CredentialBindingRefused("recovery-required", request=request)
451:                 return None
452:             pair = self._load_keypair(str(record["key_ref"]))
453:             evidence = self._evidence(request, record)
454:         except CredentialBindingRefused:
455:             raise
456:         except (OSError, ValueError) as exc:
457:             raise CredentialBindingRefused("corrupt-binding", request=request) from exc
458:         return ResolvedCredential(
459:             evidence=evidence,
460:             sign_digest=lambda digest: ed25519.sign(
461:                 pair, digest.encode(), domain=_domain_prefix(request.domain)
462:             ),
463:         )
464: 
465:     def verify(
466:         self, domain: SigningDomain, public_key: str, signature: str, digest: str
467:     ) -> bool:
468:         """Verify under the custody-owned domain prefix without persistence."""
469:         try:
470:             public = ed25519.public_key_from_b64(public_key)
471:         except ValueError:
472:             return False
473:         return ed25519.verify(public, signature, digest.encode(), domain=_domain_prefix(domain))
474: 
475:     def _new_intent(
476:         self,
477:         observer: str,
478:         *,
479:         token: str,
480:         provenance: str,
481:         key_ref: str | None = None,
482:         expected_public_key: str | None = None,
483:     ) -> dict[str, object]:
484:         _exact(observer, "observer")
485:         _exact(token, "token")
486:         key_ref = key_ref or uuid.uuid4().hex
487:         self._key_dir(key_ref)
488:         return {
489:             "schema": _INTENT_SCHEMA,
490:             "namespace": self.namespace,
491:             "observer": observer,
492:             "token": token,
493:             "key_ref": key_ref,
494:             "provenance": provenance,
495:             "expected_public_key": expected_public_key,
496:             "stage": "pending",
497:         }
498: 
499:     def _pending_by_token(self, token: str) -> dict[str, object] | None:
500:         """Find a durable token reservation while holding the provider lock."""
501:         pending_dir = self._subdir("pending-v1", create=False)
502:         if pending_dir is None:
503:             return None
504:         found: dict[str, object] | None = None
505:         for path in pending_dir.iterdir():
506:             if path.suffix != ".json":
507:                 continue
508:             record = self._read_json(path)
509:             if record is None or record.get("token") != token:
510:                 continue
511:             namespace = record.get("namespace")
512:             observer = record.get("observer")
513:             if not isinstance(namespace, str) or not isinstance(observer, str):
514:                 raise TypeError("mapped pending intent lacks exact slot identity")
515:             expected_name = f"{self._slot(namespace, observer)}.json"
516:             if path.name != expected_name:
517:                 raise ValueError("mapped pending intent is stored in the wrong slot")
518:             if found is not None:
519:                 raise BindingConflict("mapped binding token owns multiple pending slots")
520:             found = record
521:         return found
522: 
523:     def _ensure_token_index(self, intent: dict[str, object]) -> None:
524:         """Publish or validate the token index for an already durable intent."""
525:         token = str(intent["token"])
526:         intents = self._subdir("intents-v1", create=True)
527:         assert intents is not None
528:         path = intents / f"{_digest(token)}.json"
529:         if _publish_no_clobber(path, _json_bytes(intent)):
530:             return
531:         existing = self._read_json(path)
532:         if existing != intent:
533:             raise BindingConflict("mapped binding token already names another intent")
534:         _fsync_directory(intents)
535: 
536:     def _publish_completion(self, intent: dict[str, object]) -> None:
537:         """Record a validated completed intent without deleting recovery evidence."""
538:         complete = dict(intent)
539:         complete["stage"] = "complete"
540:         intents = self._subdir("intents-v1", create=True)
541:         assert intents is not None
542:         completion = intents / f"{_digest(str(intent['token']))}.complete.json"
543:         if not _publish_no_clobber(completion, _json_bytes(complete)):
544:             existing = self._read_json(completion)
545:             if existing != complete:
546:                 raise BindingConflict("mapped binding completion receipt conflicts with its intent")
547:             _fsync_directory(intents)
548: 
549:     def _assert_published_intent(
550:         self,
551:         published: dict[str, object],
552:         intent: dict[str, object],
553:         *,
554:         require_token_index: bool = True,
555:     ) -> None:
556:         """Ensure a material-valid slot still names this exact retained intent."""
557:         for name in ("namespace", "observer", "key_ref", "provenance"):
558:             if published.get(name) != intent.get(name):
559:                 raise BindingConflict("published mapped binding contradicts its retained intent")
560:         expected_public = intent.get("expected_public_key")
